## RE2-DOOR-003: one-sided unlock and savestate reversal

Status: Confirmed for the listed one-sided lock, paired passages, reentry and DuckStation restores on SLUS-00421. Other locks and full room/campaign coverage remain unverified. No tracker behavior change was needed.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Test emulator: DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795 (dev)`; the bundle version was reread for this pass. Revision/source identity and flag contracts are in [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) and [RE2-SCD-001](#re2-scd-001-bounded-leon-room-metadata-and-acquisition-contracts). This extends the separate event and named-key cases in [RE2-DOOR-001](#re2-door-001-event-driven-unlock-and-savestate-reversal) and [RE2-DOOR-002](#re2-door-002-named-key-use-and-savestate-reversal).

### Pinned pair and native writer

RDT offsets below are file offsets. Runtime addresses are PS1 virtual and little-endian, never host-process addresses. Runtime source binding checks the owned source identity and relocated section pointers on every accepted capture.

| Side | Source and binding | Target | Lock fields | Clear-bit current verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Allowed | `ROOM1090.RDT`, slot 2, `00-09:8432` | `00-16` | ID `0x90`, type `0xFE` | Unlock from this side / Unlockable |
| Denied | `ROOM1160.RDT`, slot 1, `00-16:2554` | `00-09` | ID `0x90`, type `0xFF` | Locked |

The lock fields are unsigned eight-bit values with full mask `0xFF`. Both select bank 31 bit `0x90 & 0x3F = 16`. Bank 31 begins at `0x800CCB60`, width eight bytes. This bit occupies its first unsigned 32-bit word with mask `0x00008000`. A set bit establishes fresh unlock knowledge for either side, independently of whether a departed door is currently active.

The pinned native door branch at `0x8004E07C..0x8004E0B0` handles type `0xFE` without the named-item inventory lookup. It joins the shared flag-set call at `0x8004E134..0x8004E144`, which calls `0x80072ADC`. Neither of the two decoded room sources contains a direct SCD bank 31 flag-write command. Source inspection alone would not confirm that gameplay reaches the branch.

A temporary host debugger write watch at PS1 `0x800CCB60`, width four bytes, captured the actual native write after a controller Cross interaction. The word bytes changed from `10 40 40 00` to `10 C0 40 00`, unsigned values `0x00404010` to `0x0040C010`. The only changed bit in that word was bit 16. The stop address was `0x800CCB60`, signal 5. GDB register samples were `a0 = 0x800CCB60`, `a1 = 0x10`, `s0 = 0xFE`, and `ra = pc = 0x8004E144`. These are unsigned 32-bit samples with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`; code addresses and pointers in them are PS1 virtual. The registers bind the observed store to the one-sided door branch. `native-unlock-write.json` retains the complete numeric record. The watch was removed before coherent post-operation captures. A later native unlock repeated the result with no watch installed.

The tested emulator's [register definition](https://github.com/stenzek/duckstation/blob/00e299795/src/core/gdb_server.cpp#L39-L73) identifies PC as register 37; its [watch installation](https://github.com/stenzek/duckstation/blob/00e299795/src/core/gdb_server.cpp#L1067-L1109) uses debugger callbacks. These source checks support trace interpretation, not game validation.

The whole inventory at `0x800CCB68`, an opaque 44-byte block with full `0xFF` mask per byte, was unchanged across the native unlock, completed message, passage/reentry and repeat. The early acquisition bank at `0x800CC9E8`, width 32 bytes, was also unchanged across those checks. This is byte equality, not a new interpretation of every inventory field. The tested unlock consumed no inventory item.

### Captured outcomes and restoration

Expected behavior: Locked on the denied side, Unlockable on the allowed side, then Open/Unlocked after native operation. A departed side retains only fresh unlock knowledge and stays Unchecked. Earlier/later restores must replace access verdicts while exploration persists. The unchanged reader met these expectations.

| Capture | Observed outcome | RAM SHA256 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `primary-denied-ready` | 00-16 current; bit 16 clear and bound door Locked, before native interaction. | `1e38c355928078d7cce40f24c417f6d83ae3eabd0cefe20cfffb45cc5b241fb0` |
| `primary-denied-message` | Native interaction produces blocked-door handling and stays in 00-16; bit remains clear and door Locked. | `7300b100eae1c832295e96fe703474fed1c7efea2472f01ada2af1a5768b73f3` |
| `primary-allowed-ready` | 00-09 current; bit clear, live descriptor type FE, bound door Unlockable. | `dca8f56a6b43381710f1a1659f19261f7185f55c8460c87209f4a0da49e3e05e` |
| `primary-native-unlock` | Watched native write sets bit 16; current door Open/Unlocked and departed counterpart Unchecked with fresh unlock knowledge. | `b76635372ed2524f4b6d56325b5447d02826363534b3eaedc888ed8d130ea410` |
| `primary-unlock-complete` | Message acknowledged in 00-09; unlock retained, inventory/acquisition unchanged. | `c745e924daf309379c2f3962a44a3f882ee08c491b111283473bc51775b00812` |
| `primary-outbound` | Native 00-09 to 00-16 passage; formerly denied counterpart now Open/Unlocked. | `ec5a55a26b70487e256c142f3345ed6a5937fad9a292fef8688e57c9eea929f3` |
| `primary-return` | Native 00-16 to 00-09 return; current allowed-side door Open/Unlocked. | `419f30a92293536e7084845c0867c1ee3398449b7403271c9fe0f1ffb6d157e0` |
| `primary-reentry` | Native 00-09 to 00-16 reentry; counterpart remains Open/Unlocked. | `1829424861b8e26f630816465ff674a94427c8a36d297e60fe3d6b336efd325e` |
| `rewind-primary-allowed` | Actual earlier allowed-side load restores Unlockable and original RAM bytes; visits retained. | `dca8f56a6b43381710f1a1659f19261f7185f55c8460c87209f4a0da49e3e05e` |
| `rewind-primary-denied` | Actual earlier denied-side load restores Locked and original RAM bytes; visits retained. | `1e38c355928078d7cce40f24c417f6d83ae3eabd0cefe20cfffb45cc5b241fb0` |
| `restore-primary-unlocked` | Actual later load restores Open/Unlocked and completed-use RAM bytes; visits retained. | `c745e924daf309379c2f3962a44a3f882ee08c491b111283473bc51775b00812` |
| `repeat-primary-allowed` | Another actual earlier load reproduces the allowed-side baseline RAM bytes. | `dca8f56a6b43381710f1a1659f19261f7185f55c8460c87209f4a0da49e3e05e` |
| `repeat-primary-unlock` | Native unlock repeated without a debugger watch; bit set, Open/Unlocked, inventory/acquisition unchanged. | `ecbde0cc0f88faa41b47a4d8b5d1b66191a9f173c0b3787d2de6d8c3aa261eff` |

The denied preparation's word was `0x00004000`; the allowed preparation's was `0x00404010`. They start from independent previously captured agent checkpoints and differ in other native progression flags. Each restore is compared with its own original baseline. They are not a single uninterrupted navigation sequence between preparations. The continuous outbound/return/reentry sequence begins after the allowed-side native unlock.

`audit.py` requires 28 accepted captures and compares every replayed publication with the original capture's map state. For the two current sources it checks exact source-offset binding, SCE ID 1, lock ID `0x90` and type FE/FF. It verifies current-side verdicts, departed-side uncertainty, retained visits, the native-writer register record, unchanged inventory/acquisition and four byte-identical restore comparisons. None of these checks assert that a source-only departed door is currently passable.

The pass has fifteen accepted sources: `01-00`, `01-0a`, `01-0b`, `00-16`, `01-04`, `01-05`, `01-07`, `00-0c`, `00-14`, `00-13`, `00-12`, `00-10`, `00-0f`, `00-0a` and `00-09`. Eight are new relative to RE2-DOOR-002's nineteen-source union, bringing the recorded union to twenty-seven. `coverage.json` derives the union from the prior accepted lists. Source acceptance is not complete item/door validation, native outline coverage or a campaign percentage.

A separate production history-file save/close/reopen retains those fifteen source visits, sixteen map-region visits and the agent playthrough identity. Restored history initially has no current room, no door observations and only Unknown item records. Fresh polls of the two earlier captures and the later capture reconstruct Unlockable, Locked and Open respectively, with the same visits. This is a file round trip over real captures, not a live runner restart. The emulator loads are independently recorded above. Memory-card persistence is not tested.

### Preparation, reproduction and rejected attempts

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-one-sided-20261019/`. These temporary local references are not portable public fixtures. Raw RAM, savestates, owned game data and diagnostic helpers remain outside Git. Before boot, both achievement integrations were false, cards were None, no per-game overrides were present and the existing black display shader was enabled. A private screenshot's central half had zero minimum and maximum channel values. No gameplay image was emitted. The agent history file was isolated from the user's playthrough.

The denied preparation starts from RE2-DOOR-001's `access-unlocked.sav`, SHA256 `dfd5be7bb5db71424ce99ad5800bcc897237011298ef40900c60286e2149a77c`. Native passages reach `01-0a`, `01-0b` and `00-16`. The accepted `01-0b` approach to `00-16` is X = -649, Y = 0, Z = -24332, yaw = 520, from `ROOM1160.RDT`'s return entry at file offset 2522 with reversed facing. In `00-16`, the controlled denied-side placement is X = -26159, Y = -7200, Z = -25126, yaw = 2048, floor = 4. Its height/floor and entry position come from `ROOM1090.RDT` offset 8432. One native Cross produces the denied interaction without changing bit 16.

The allowed preparation loads RE2-DOOR-002's `key-present-locked.sav`, SHA256 `e530308cd0b06eb0a9353790b9f7c3bfe9fc02f62d00c256c8c88a4dab827818`. Position-assisted native passages reach `01-05 -> 01-07 -> 00-0c -> 00-14 -> 00-13 -> 00-12 -> 00-10 -> 00-0f -> 00-0a -> 00-09`. A separate native named-key use on this route sets bit 9 and reduces that key's quantity. It is preparation, not the confirmed one-sided operation. The target bit 16 stays clear throughout. `allowed-route-approaches.json` records source entry packets used to prepare the route; actual successful interactions and failed attempts are distinguished in the captures and notes.

The accepted allowed-side stance is X = -13894, Y = 0, Z = -25290, yaw = 96, floor = 0 in `00-09`, from `ROOM1160.RDT` offset 2554 with reversed facing. Save while paused, then use native Cross/K. The watch records the first flag change. Remove it, resume and let the unlock handling finish before taking a coherent capture. A later Cross acknowledges the message, and a further Cross enters `00-16`. Its native entry is X = -26159, Y = -7200, Z = -25126, floor = 4. Face yaw 2048 there for the native return; face yaw 96 at the original first-side stance for reentry. Wait until each transition settles before accepting a capture. These are position-assisted interaction tests, not visual marker/facing alignment or uninterrupted ordinary navigation.

The agent checkpoints are `primary-allowed-locked.sav`, SHA256 `ace1efb3c8ec9b0ab3b8aabaa61bf980a1ec3425ed447e89d385323677f9a31a`; `primary-denied-locked.sav`, SHA256 `5543b1a8769a33f0eb8d6119e1d486dd23c1568bc9c2c06a63c470368a948ee4`; and `primary-allowed-unlocked.sav`, SHA256 `d53939aa01ef1e52d9bfc25c6503dac35740ec77626291b8c5cd8160bedd304d`. Restore through the isolated selected slot with native F1. The repeat restores the allowed-side baseline and performs native use/message acknowledgment without installing a watch.

Setup retains the earlier agent checkpoints' high-health and 255-round infinite-magazine assistance from RE2-LIVE-002/003. No new health/ammunition patch, enemy-state edit or acquisition/key/story/inventory write was made. Direct writes are limited to the position/facing, body-height and floor fields already recorded in RE2-DOOR-001/002. The additional upper-floor placement in `00-0c` uses Y = -3600/floor = 2 from `ROOM1140.RDT` offset 2518. `position-edits.jsonl` records every direct write, and `placement-provenance.json` identifies the source packets and rejected setup. This does not establish natural stair movement or a general Y-to-floor formula.

The initial candidate `01-02:7066` was interrupted by its native sequence, which exited through separate slot 20 with bit 15 still clear. It did not establish operation of that one-sided lock and is excluded as unlock evidence. `rejected-candidate.json` retains the five corresponding capture labels. A fresh private tracker starts the confirmed case. The provisional `denied-side-entry` was still `01-0b`, not `00-16`; its following upper-floor placement was reversed before accepted entry. Mandatory source checks were then added to the private placement commands. Later capture-client restarts seeded only listed accepted observations through the unchanged reader, without opening user history.

Enemy contact interrupted several `00-10` approach attempts and changed position/facing. Those attempts establish neither passage nor enemy kills. Native input diagnostics confirmed the configured controls; their unpaused samples were never tracker publications. The accepted exit followed stable native control at a distant entry and then a placement at X = 445, Y = -7200, Z = -22634, yaw = 0, floor = 4. Only the accepted `west-0f` capture proves the subsequent passage. These observations do not confirm the renderer's yaw conversion.

After testing, the isolated emulator was stopped and its original settings and selected test slot restored byte for byte. `cleanup.json` records both restored hashes. Other one-sided and named-key locks, other event conditions, natural conditional floors, visual heading/marker alignment and an uninterrupted ordinary-navigation route remain outside this confirmation. No normal-save or full-campaign claim is made.

## RE2-PLAYER-001: native movement confirms continuous heading

Status: Confirmed for the continuous facing conversion relative to the pinned map axes, eight native forward-movement pairs in two rooms, and the listed DuckStation restores on SLUS-00421. Player-position projection, door placement and conditional floors remain unverified. No production behavior or UI change was needed.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Test emulator: DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795 (dev)`; the bundle version was reread. The runtime signature remains `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. Source identity and pinned projection axes are in [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) and [RE2-MAP-001](#re2-map-001-native-leon-floor-selectors-and-numeric-contours). This changes the latter claim's former unverified continuous-heading limit, not its other candidate map findings.

### Measured direction and rendered arrow

All addresses here are PS1 virtual, little-endian. Facing at `0x800C7D9A` is an unsigned 16-bit value, width two bytes; the angle mask is `0x0FFF`. World X/Y/Z at `0x800C7D5C/0x800C7D60/0x800C7D64` are signed 32-bit fields, width four bytes each and full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. These fields were read, never written in this pass. Stage and room at `0x800CC948/0x800CC94A` are unsigned 16-bit fields, width two bytes and full mask `0xFFFF`.

The pinned projection increases map X with world X and decreases map Y with world Z. Consequently, native forward displacement supplies an independent clockwise-from-north reference through `atan2(deltaX, deltaZ)`. Each accepted pair keeps its source, height, yaw, health and sampled player-action block unchanged while moving more than 500 world units. Collision-limited samples and the enemy-interrupted setup are excluded below. Expected behavior was agreement with the continuous conversion already recorded in RE2-MAP-001. The unchanged reader met that expectation.

| Before → after | Raw yaw | Delta X, Z | Published heading | Movement heading | Difference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `hall-facing-2` → `hall-walk-2` | 4208 | 1578, -287 | 99.843750° | 100.308042° | 0.464292° |
| `hall-facing-3b` → `hall-walk-3` | 5328 | -494, -1411 | 198.281250° | 199.295472° | 1.014222° |
| `hall-facing-4` → `hall-walk-4` | 6288 | -554, 119 | 282.656250° | 282.123006° | 0.533244° |
| `hall-facing-5` → `hall-walk-5` | 7248 | 65, 556 | 7.031250° | 6.667980° | 0.363270° |
| `shop2-facing-0` → `shop2-walk-0` | 779 | 204, -528 | 158.466797° | 158.875281° | 0.408484° |
| `shop2-facing-1` → `shop2-walk-1` | 1739 | -505, -260 | 242.841797° | 242.758258° | 0.083538° |
| `shop2-facing-2` → `shop2-walk-2` | 2699 | -352, 536 | 327.216797° | 326.706437° | 0.510360° |
| `shop2-facing-3` → `shop2-walk-3` | 3739 | 479, 289 | 58.623047° | 58.895727° | 0.272680° |

The first four pairs use source `01-00`; the other four use `00-01`. The raw values above one revolution confirm masking the yaw independently of its upper bits. Native Right turns increase the stored yaw and wrap the published heading through north; a separate native Left turn in `00-01` changes yaw 3739 to 2699 and heading 58.623046875° to 327.216796875°, with the same position and health. The measurements confirm the sign and offset with an observed maximum difference of 1.014222°, rounded to six decimal places. They do not establish zero-error movement physics or a universal two-degree guarantee.

[The numeric regression references](../../tests/fixtures/re2/heading-movements.json) retain these eight pairs, raw yaw, displacement, the captured published heading and both evidence hashes. The synthetic reader regression checks those published values against the independently measured movement vector with a two-degree bound. A separate fresh-yaw regression alternates the captured earlier/later angles without losing visits. The bound accommodates the observed difference; it is not a new production tolerance or an inferred heading value.

The browser regression replays the eight captured headings through the unchanged page with explicitly synthetic visits. It transforms the SVG path's actual first point, the arrow tip, into screen coordinates and compares its direction with the measured movement. This covers the glyph orientation and SVG rotation, including pose-only updates. All 133 outline and exact-door-binding browser checks also pass. It is an automated rendering replay, not another native gameplay observation. No native-camera or in-game map screenshot comparison was performed. At this baseline the marker identifies only the current room. RE2-PLAYER-002 later adds guarded approximate placement.

### Publications, restoration and evidence

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-facing-20261019/`. `audit.py` requires exactly 31 listed accepted publications and compares every replayed production map state with its originally recorded publication. Full paused captures use `.ram`; bounded paused production reads use `.reads.json`, recording every consumed address, width and returned bytes plus pose/health samples. The latter replay requires an exact recorded range and agreeing overlapping data, with no fallback bytes. The eight-pair reference hashes identify RAM bytes for `hall-facing-2`/`hall-walk-2`, and exact UTF-8 read-transcript bytes for the other pairs. Those private bytes and new savestates stay outside Git. Only numeric test references are committed.

Actual native F1 loads reproduce both original RAM images byte for byte:

| Baseline / loaded capture | Yaw / published heading | RAM SHA256 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `hall-earlier` / `hall-rewind` | 4208 / 99.84375° | `725ea23cb5355f7b42b996765650ded6e4bf8387d1a1aba8733ab6d1eeee97bb` |
| `hall-later` / `hall-restore` | 7248 / 7.03125° | `6294df89d2f5166563abf5853e6c29f0f9fd6082997c7d5c39f3d6fb8437b390` |

Both loads retain the two agent source visits and replace the heading from fresh state. A separate production history-file save/close/reopen starts with no current room or heading, retains the same two visits/playthrough identity, and reconstructs earlier, later and second-room headings from fresh captures. This is an offline file round trip, not a live runner restart or memory-card persistence.

The audit also checks byte equality throughout each primary checkpoint sequence for inventory at `0x800CCB68`, width 44 bytes; early acquisition at `0x800CC9E8`, width 32 bytes; and bank 31 at `0x800CCB60`, width eight bytes. These are opaque blocks with full `0xFF` mask per byte here. Health samples at `0x800C7E7A` are signed 16-bit, width two bytes and full mask `0xFFFF`; the player-action sample at `0x800C7D24` is an opaque eight-byte block, full mask `0xFF` per byte. The eight accepted movement pairs retain health 28447 in `01-00` and 32377 in `00-01`, and action bytes `01 00 00 40 01 00 01 00`. This does not add a general interpretation of every sampled action or inventory field.

No new room source was accepted beyond the prior twenty-seven-source union. Publication, outline and test counts do not measure complete room item/door readiness or campaign progress.

### Reproduction, exclusions and cleanup

Before boot, both achievement integrations were false, cards were None, audio was muted, no per-game overrides were present and the black display shader's hash matched the earlier passes. A suppressed private screenshot's central half had zero minimum and maximum channel values. No game image was emitted. The private tracker and history file were separate from the user's exploration history. Earlier checkpoints retain the previously recorded high-health and infinite-magazine assistance from RE2-LIVE-002/003. No new patch or guest-memory write was made, including position, facing, health, ammunition, enemy, inventory, acquisition, key or story fields.

The first preparation loads RE2-LIVE-002's `ammo-both.sav`, SHA256 `89e6315c5c8138784c9a27b138e90c78d4520107d1467b2f6b79d8f7baa7e570`. Its preliminary yaw-3088 forward pair moved X/Z by 9/556 with unchanged health, agreeing within 0.4789°. A subsequent turn suffered enemy contact: health fell 32377 → 32317 and position changed. `shop-facing-1`, RAM SHA256 `de43e2818309dd675762144bcd5271faf504c9722672395e8da8140f1bfa4a05`, is excluded as turn/direction evidence. Native Right turns and a 700 ms forward hold reach the accepted yaw-4208 start/end pair. Save the paused earlier state with native F2. Continue with native Right turns, a 700 ms forward hold at yaw 5328 and 240 ms holds at yaw 6288 and 7248. The first turn attempted after a private capture-client reconnect produced no yaw change and supplies no turn evidence; the repeated turn after explicit resume succeeded. Raw yaw, rather than an assumed exact timed angle, identifies each measurement. A final Left hold of 440 ms supplies the opposite-turn check. No pickup, new passage or floor change is required for either primary sequence.

## RE2-PLAYER-002: approximate position follows native pose and restores

Status: Confirmed for coherent pose publication, guarded outline placement, the listed native movements and DuckStation restores in two rooms on SLUS-00421. Absolute in-game visual alignment and untested position contexts remain Candidate. This adds an approximate moving marker; it does not claim complete positioning coverage.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Test emulator: DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795 (dev)`; the bundle version was reread. Runtime signature SHA256 is `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) pins identity, [RE2-MAP-001](#re2-map-001-native-leon-floor-selectors-and-numeric-contours) records the source-established projection and [RE2-PLAYER-001](#re2-player-001-native-movement-confirms-continuous-heading) records the independent facing checks. The previous implementation displayed only a room marker despite having the world origin and projection metadata.

### Placement and coherent input

Addresses are PS1 virtual, little-endian. X/Y/Z start at `0x800C7D5C`, a 12-byte block of three signed 32-bit fields at offsets 0/4/8, each with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Facing at `0x800C7D9A` is unsigned 16-bit, width two bytes, angle mask `0x0FFF`. Both must fit the initial 352-byte player block at `0x800C7CE0`; profile loading rejects otherwise. The floor byte at `0x800C7E2A`, width one, mask `0xFF`, selects the existing native region and remains part of final coherence checks. Y is captured with the pose; placement uses X/Z and the established floor/region selection.

A single required projection implementation now serves runtime player placement and generated door centers. It preserves the fractional result of the translation/scale recorded in RE2-MAP-001 for smooth approximate movement; the native game's integer draw coordinates quantize that result. Bias and scale are required validated configuration. Each region's required world origin is a pair of signed 16-bit values from the pinned map descriptor. Missing/malformed origin metadata and invalid scale fail during loading; no guessed point or sample configuration is substituted.

The projected point must lie strictly inside the currently selected region's even/odd floor fill. This retains holes and disconnected islands. Boundary, outside and unmapped points produce an explicit `RoomOnlyPosition` with a reason. Placement never borrows another region's floor or moves an unsupported point to an arbitrary nearby location. The existing page labels supported positions approximate and other positions room-only; pose-only updates retain the marker node, pan and zoom. Interrupted tracking removes the marker until a fresh publication returns.

Position and facing come from the same initial player block. They can change during later validation without forcing Retry. Stage/room halfwords at `0x800CC948/0x800CC94A`, each unsigned 16-bit with full mask `0xFFFF`, are sampled before the player block and compared with the save-block identity, then checked again through the usual coherence pass. A controlled synthetic whole-room change between block reads verifies rejection before publication. Floor/context/source checks remain mandatory. Failed or short reads do not partially replace the previous tracker state. This is bounded read coherence, not a claim that the entire game is frozen to one frame.

### Native observations and restores

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-position-20261019/`. Raw RAM, consumed-read transcripts, new savestates and owned game data stay outside Git. [Numeric test references](../../tests/fixtures/re2/position-observations.json) retain world coordinates, marker results and evidence hashes for these five observations:

| Capture | Source | World X, Y, Z | Published marker / map X, Y | Evidence SHA256 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `first-unplaced` | `00-01` | -16222, 0, -12512 | room | `076f19a312bc712eda5e8c1839a4e62c22861faea4f16010fc27485f6c3544cf` |
| `first-earlier` | `00-01` | -16012, 0, -13488 | approximate 97.640000, 79.751111 | `3e39021fc5dfb93baf86ec540049bd094ff8f15383c661906147b2289f7e28ad` |
| `first-later` | `00-01` | -16192, 0, -13729 | approximate 97.240000, 80.286667 | `875fc1120549357cb8c07ea6d9a0e39225953d4ff92b0b2bc7e5cd3b0869b86e` |
| `second-earlier` | `01-00` | -13349, -1800, 1812 | approximate 150.557778, 102.751111 | `05a27fb7b59979e77ae61d645d9c4251728c4bb5bec91f4aeb205d7544e7a282` |
| `second-later` | `01-00` | -13423, -1800, 302 | approximate 150.393333, 106.106667 | `e8d7b43ef987fa227b1fde8da14096279f86f7a424d9cff13b537a0d14c8fa12` |

`first-unplaced` is outside the drawn floor under the continuous native projection, so retaining room-only is the expected result. Native forward input enters a supported interior and changes the marker between the first earlier/later points. Collision deflection means its actual X/Z movement differs from an ideal unobstructed forward vector; placement follows the sampled coordinates. The second sequence turns natively, then walks between its two interior points. No position/facing edit was made in this pass.

There are sixteen accepted paused publications. `.ram` evidence hashes identify full paused RAM bytes; `first-unplaced` uses the exact UTF-8 `.reads.json` consumed-read transcript. The other four table entries use `.ram`. The private range replay requires recorded bytes with agreeing overlaps and no fallback data. Actual native F1 loads reproduce all four full RAM images byte for byte: `first-earlier` → `first-rewind`, `first-later` → `first-restored`, `second-earlier` → `second-rewind`, and `second-later` → `second-restored`. Each loaded marker point agrees with its baseline, while visits accumulated up to that load remain retained.

The unpaused probe makes sixty production polls at a requested 10 Hz while native walking occurs. All sixty return Display, with eight distinct initial-block poses; every point remains inside the second region. Median poll time is 18.576 ms and maximum is 21.461 ms. Every complete consumed-read sequence replays to its original publication, and the position is independently checked against the pinned source constants and the pose bytes from that poll's initial player block. No extra pose read is used to pretend that a later frame was the published frame. These observations establish normal-motion publication for this sequence, not an uninterrupted navigation route or all transition/load timings.

A separate production history-file save/close/reopen retains both source visits and the playthrough identity, begins without a current room or reconstructed point, and reproduces all four positions through fresh polls. It is an offline file round trip, not a live runner restart or normal memory-card persistence. Position remains transient game state, not stored exploration knowledge.

### Preparation and reproducibility

Before boot, both achievement integrations were false, both cards were None, audio was muted, no per-game overrides were present, and the existing black shader hash matched the earlier passes. A suppressed private screenshot's central half was entirely black. The agent tracker and history were isolated from the user's playthrough. The checkpoints inherit high-health and infinite-magazine assistance from RE2-LIVE-002/003 and earlier position-assisted preparation; this pass adds no debugger RAM writes or new patches to position, facing, health, ammunition, enemy, inventory, acquisition, key or story fields.

The first sequence loads RE2-LIVE-002's `shop-ready.sav`, SHA256 `e91351c6e55f6db2c87c4d0cca0a38c32197238097c0d57c69054cd696093528`, through the isolated selected slot. Its initial pose is the table's unplaced state, yaw 779. Native Up holds of 700 ms and then 240 ms produce the two listed interior states. Pause and capture each state; save each with native F2. Prepare the selected slot from each private checkpoint, use native F1, then capture again while paused. Timed input is a reproduction aid, not a guarantee of an exact displacement.

The second sequence loads RE2-PLAYER-001's `hall-heading-earlier.sav`, SHA256 `8b87722743255f85c608cd5ec1c0b9f0345fba23cf5405e54562715c8b26d77d`. Its initial X/Y/Z are -13349/-1800/1812, raw yaw 4208. A native Right hold of 440 ms reaches yaw 5168 without changing position. Save the paused earlier state. Start the unpaused read-only probe, which explicitly resumes after connecting, then hold native Up for 700 ms. The final pause/capture supplies the second later point. Save it and perform both actual earlier/later loads. No pickup, unlock or new room is required for either sequence.

Agent checkpoint SHA256 values are `first-earlier.sav` = `ca6e6bd324c93d814376c4b9c9fbf2eb7a5043b72bf6cc8dcff80ee81edc3b0e`, `first-later.sav` = `e30bbd4d1cfd8adbf1e77c265a77e8139a934b85f44fcaea8def8a5f6036c120`, `second-earlier.sav` = `ce257adbfe5335168a1d72a0942b200652facec26ae0fa9bf21c229795d706c0` and `second-later.sav` = `6a1ef73450d0b7ab7ee5c1d43d3c7428a5ee7c63fa9efef942405ff000a879fb`. Health remains 32377 in the first sequence and 28447 in the second, sampled as signed 16-bit at `0x800C7E7A`, width two, full mask `0xFFFF`. Inventory at `0x800CCB68` (44 bytes), early acquisition at `0x800CC9E8` (32 bytes) and bank 31 at `0x800CCB60` (eight bytes) are byte-identical throughout each checkpoint sequence; these are opaque blocks with full `0xFF` per-byte masks here.

`baseline/src` is an export of commit `63f42ac`. Separate old/new-reader replays compare the sixteen new paused publications and the 31 RE2-PLAYER-001 publications. All 47 agree after removing only the intentional `player_position` field difference; item, door, completion, heading, region and exploration outputs remain identical. This is regression replay, not 47 newly played situations. `replay.py <source-path> <output-json>` produces each comparison side; `audit.py <repository>/src` checks the saved comparisons, native/older evidence, live sequences, numeric references and history round trip. These temporary local helpers are not portable public fixtures.

Shared-projection regeneration reproduces room metadata SHA256 `fbeb843f93a01e7407f11d3168ed30270314bf38de247dc39b7ef035b817b29b` and floor-plan metadata SHA256 `40b5599e5558237c5986aba310bbc7bfebb376fce48a3700ee1b75e3070da93c` byte for byte. No door-marker placement or catalog outcome changes are introduced by extracting the formula. The refreshed README PNG replays only RE2-LIVE-001's original opening capture, RAM SHA256 `c9665284ca828e618971721be518ed1197c4ea3d3e210b0fffa9017573b061a7`, with one explored source and the approximate marker. No game screenshot or later map was shown in chat.

After testing, the isolated emulator and private map preview were stopped. Settings and selected slot were restored byte for byte: SHA256 `792443989dc8f5f8ee3dabcc2a20dd71a3e61cc0a44de8a84266d51e6e889069` and `995bb0ff5df6eb6b254d87ea9c6af5386105a50bfd50cdab018da50465965bfa`, recorded in `cleanup.json`. The hidden preview tab was closed. No new source was added to the prior twenty-seven-source runtime union. Counts of captures, polls and tests do not establish whole-room or campaign readiness. Absolute native visual alignment, other position contexts, door placement and natural conditional-floor transitions remain outside this confirmation.

## RE2-FLOOR-001: native same-room floor changes and restores

Status: Confirmed for two native descents, two ascents and four paused DuckStation restores in source `00-16` on SLUS-00421. This confirms the listed floor-dependent map selections and separate exploration of their two regions. Other conditional floors and story-controlled map alternatives remain Candidate. No production or UI change was needed.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`; the bundle version was reread. Runtime signature SHA256 remains `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) pins identity. [RE2-MAP-001](#re2-map-001-native-leon-floor-selectors-and-numeric-contours) established the selectors offline; this pass addresses its unverified natural floor changes for one source.

### Observed floor selection and exploration

All addresses here are PS1 virtual, little-endian. The floor at `0x800C7E2A` is an unsigned byte, full mask `0xFF`. Stage/room at `0x800CC948/0x800CC94A` are unsigned halfwords, each width two and full mask `0xFFFF`. X/Y/Z at `0x800C7D5C` are three signed 32-bit values at offsets 0/4/8, each width four and full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Facing at `0x800C7D9A` is unsigned 16-bit, width two, angle mask `0x0FFF`. Position/facing and floor use the existing bounded snapshot and final-context checks from [RE2-PLAYER-002](#re2-player-002-approximate-position-follows-native-pose-and-restores).

The room source remains `00-16` while the game changes floor 4 to 1 during descent and 1 to 4 during ascent. Floor 4 selects `re2-03:0D`; floor 1 selects `re2-02:11`. The tracker retains one source visit and two independent map-region visits. A fresh live-probe tracker initially reveals only the starting region. The second region appears only after its first accepted native floor publication. Reversing the current floor, including through an earlier state, retains both explored regions. These observations establish neither the source's complete item list nor clearance.

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-floors-20261019/`. [Numeric references](../../tests/fixtures/re2/floor-transitions.json) contain only source/floor/pose/map expectations and evidence hashes. The table hashes identify full paused 2 MiB RAM images, kept outside Git.

| Capture | World X, Y, Z | Floor | Published area:region | RAM SHA256 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `native-upper-approach` | -21999, -7200, -20699 | 4 | `re2-03:0D` | `4cd1c4352798dabca7c9a65ea79eea69776d9983ca1699e2033c7a76c5acc075` |
| `lower-later` | -14499, -1800, -20699 | 1 | `re2-02:11` | `d29e8256dedf132fb1f8e2b0af7296941bb064787e2d3d73f99079476faa3ce7` |
| `upper-return` | -22191, -7200, -20606 | 4 | `re2-03:0D` | `fe322fbcf774b676b7bed0b4d65930733a2410bf0ab347c3ac884c95829c53da` |
| `repeat-lower` | -14499, -1800, -20699 | 1 | `re2-02:11` | `f8f36dffefb942ef1099462273d5ad7cd48e3894597e7c50c863ef51d4f09691` |
| `repeat-upper` | -22198, -7200, -20743 | 4 | `re2-03:0D` | `0df6103d287ae15abcbf127a9d8b8c2ccc47edb3037aea34feb7e24ae85442f2` |

The first descent uses native Up and Cross held together. Native turning prepares the reverse direction; the same input combination ascends. After native loads, independent live probes repeat both directions. Their accepted initial-block poses and floor bytes agree with the map selections above. All listed end positions retain an explicit room-only marker because their projection is outside the mapped floor. No absolute position-alignment claim is added.

Four paused native F1 loads reproduce full RAM byte for byte: `native-upper-approach` → `upper-rewind-paused`, `lower-later` → `lower-restore`, `upper-return` → `upper-restore`, and `lower-later` → `lower-restore-repeat`. Fresh reader publications select the restored floor while the accumulated two-region exploration remains intact. A separate production history-file save/close/reopen preserves one source, both regions and the playthrough identity, begins without a current room or reconstructed position, and recovers each current floor from fresh captures. This is an offline history round trip, not a running-tracker restart or normal memory-card persistence.

### Live reads, browser checks and limits

There are two separate 100-poll production-reader probes at a requested 10 Hz. Descent publishes all 100 observations, 35 on floor 4 and 65 on floor 1, with 27 distinct accepted poses. Ascent publishes 98, 47 on floor 1 and 51 on floor 4, with 28 distinct accepted poses. Its indices 47 and 60 return `Game state changed during read`; the previous tracker is unchanged and no partial map is published. Exact sequential replay consumes every recorded read and reproduces all 200 outcomes and all accepted map states. Floor, XYZ and yaw are checked against that poll's initial player block, without a later diagnostic read standing in for its frame.

Median poll times are 13.501 ms for descent and 14.395 ms for ascent. Maximum times are 974.802 ms and 60.067 ms respectively. The long descent read is retained in the evidence. Requested polling frequency is not a claim of sustained 10 Hz publication during every transition.

Thirty paused observations return Display and one doorway-transition observation returns Retry. These include preparation and unsuccessful approaches, not thirty floor transitions. The private audit replays four explicitly listed controller-session groups so their original visit histories agree. Health is 26767 across twelve full case captures. Inventory at `0x800CCB68` for 44 bytes, early acquisition at `0x800CC9E8` for 32 bytes, and bank 31 at `0x800CCB60` for eight bytes remain identical across those captures. These are opaque byte blocks here, with full `0xFF` per-byte masks. Health is signed 16-bit at `0x800C7E7A`, width two, full mask `0xFFFF`.

The synthetic reader regression uses the native numeric contexts with synthetic source/signature proofs and descriptors. It checks same-source region accumulation and fresh floor reversal; it is not additional gameplay. The browser regression constructs synthetic visits using those contexts. It checks one initial floor option, entry-only exposure of the second region, one explored outline per floor, automatic following, retained manual browsing/view across player floor changes and restores, and Follow player. The browser is not attached to live emulator memory during this regression. The UI and README image remain unchanged.

No new source is added to the prior twenty-seven-source runtime union. This resolves one natural floor-change case, not all conditional floors, story-dependent selectors, item/door outcomes, absolute position alignment or a complete route using ordinary navigation. Poll, test and publication counts do not measure campaign readiness.

### Preparation, exclusions and reproduction

Before both boots, Cheevos and Achievements were disabled, cards were None, audio was muted and the existing black display shader was enabled and hash-checked. No per-game overrides were added. Private suppressed screenshots before and after the input-binding restart had entirely black central halves. No game image was emitted. Agent exploration stayed separate; the user's history was not opened. Prior checkpoints retain recorded high-health, infinite-magazine and position/floor preparation assistance. This pass makes no debugger RAM writes or new health, ammunition, position, floor, enemy, acquisition, inventory, key or story patches.

The first preparation loads RE2-DOOR-003's `primary-denied-locked.sav`, SHA256 `5543b1a8769a33f0eb8d6119e1d486dd23c1568bc9c2c06a63c470368a948ee4`. Walking, running and action-only attempts retain floor 4; these are unsuccessful approaches and establish no natural transition. The independent native-doorway preparation loads `primary-allowed-unlocked.sav`, SHA256 `d53939aa01ef1e52d9bfc25c6503dac35740ec77626291b8c5cd8160bedd304d`. Hold the existing O repeated-Cross macro for 2500 ms, then let the doorway transition settle. `native-door-entry` captures the transition's Retry; `native-upper-entry` captures coherent `00-16`, XYZ -26159/-7200/-25126, floor 4 and yaw 0. Its RAM SHA256 is `d970b2635d8b4a64f153237ecb6ab47e74c39ade7ba9a9923472cd83628d7e93`.

The owned `ROOM1160.RDT` has SHA256 `d2d7b559870858710a17d34a99b449bf93792ad51a3f1c3b156a1d58a6725d7c`. The pinned OpenBiohazard2 [SCA parser](https://github.com/OpenBiohazard2/OpenBiohazard2/blob/fd525d4b80539b933bea3146069204df6f8c0d0f/fileio/rdt_sca.go#L25-L82) supplied shape/orientation leads for a narrower approach. Its interpretation is not confirmation of this revision's movement behavior. The owned source's stair record at file offset 2124 spans X -21548 with width 5880 and Z -21287 with depth 1468. The reference approach is X -21999, Y -7200, Z -20699, yaw 0, floor 4.

From native upper entry, Up reaches the nearby boundary; turn Left toward positive Z, walk to the landing's positive-Z limit, then turn Right and walk toward the stair end. Shift slightly toward negative Z and face positive X. The recorded native inputs, separated by paused pose checks and explicit resume, are Up 3000 ms, O 1000 ms, Up 2500 ms; Left 470 ms and Up 3000 ms; Right 470 ms and Up 1600 ms; Right 470 ms, Up 180 ms, Left 470 ms and Up 2200 ms; then U 2200 ms. U is the existing Up/Square binding. These final ordinary-input attempts still retain floor 4. Save the approach while paused with native F2. Timings are reproduction aids, not guaranteed displacement or angles.

The computer-use API rejected an attempted Up/Cross chord before sending input because it permits only one ordinary key per chord. Stop the isolated emulator, then add test-only Macro4, Keyboard/B, binding Up & Cross with frequency zero, to its existing controller sections. Boot the saved native approach with achievements disabled and the black shader still enabled. B held for 2400 ms supplies the first descent. Native Right 940 ms then Left 80 ms prepares yaw 2048 in the measured reverse pass; B 2400 ms supplies the ascent. Exact observed yaw is used, not assumed from timing. The repeated ascent preparation initially measured yaw 2000 and used an additional native Right hold of 30 ms before B; its final yaw is 2048.

Agent checkpoint SHA256 values are `native-upper-approach.sav` = `ae5ba79981c64fb0a997713c1a1945c4aebbea1bdd1c8db7e6ac22b22b9d8450`, `lower-later.sav` = `1852313fa915bebe22db8e17c14296fbfd7931282bcecec1c44f407a1d2ecee7`, and `upper-return.sav` = `834e39b6b7d31402ff8a4c3d866c7858b9160a4ce7513dac40a9c7a5398af193`. `upper-earlier.sav`, SHA256 `222be357dad728eaed3b6d4ca3d9231d69451fc56580ecc58d216a2fe65ae823`, is an unused approach checkpoint, not restore evidence.

A preliminary `upper-rewind` load made while running restores the expected floor/pose, but its later RAM capture differs from `native-upper-approach` in 2278 bytes after the unpaused interval. It is excluded from byte-identical restore evidence. Subsequent loads occur while paused and pass all four exact comparisons. This distinction does not imply that running loads must reproduce every transient byte at a later read.

`audit.py` replays the bounded/full paused evidence, all sequential live reads, numeric references and history reconstruction. Its separate session-group boundaries and the preliminary observations are retained in `audit.json`; `browser-states.json` retains map publications reconstructed from fresh native captures. ## RE2-SESSION-001: live tracker restarts and restores

Status: Confirmed for the listed item, named door and two floor contexts across four clean tracker restarts and eleven native loads on SLUS-00421. This confirms running CLI/history/HTTP integration for these cases. It does not confirm crash recovery, normal saves, other locks or other conditional floors. No production or UI change was needed.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`; the bundle version was reread. Runtime signature SHA256 remains `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. Identity is pinned in [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout). Earlier offline history round trips in [RE2-COLLECT-003](#re2-collect-003-scripted-activation-preserves-collection-identity), [RE2-DOOR-002](#re2-door-002-named-key-use-and-savestate-reversal) and [RE2-FLOOR-001](#re2-floor-001-native-same-room-floor-changes-and-restores) did not test an actual tracker restart. This pass addresses that gap using their existing checkpoints.

### Running tracker observations

Five distinct OS processes run the unchanged `tools/track.py` with the real RE2 profile, test GDB port 2346, requested 10 Hz, `--map`, an explicit private log and the same private `--history-file`. No tracker, history, memory reader or HTTP implementation is patched in these native runs. The helper reads the actual `/state` endpoint. Each process ends with SIGINT, exit zero, normal GDB shutdown and release of the history lock/map port before the next start. These are four clean process restarts, not four calls to `main()` within one interpreter.

The helper samples HTTP state at roughly 10 Hz until twenty consecutive responses match the expected loaded case. Fourteen stages produce 281 responses: 280 matching settled samples and one initial `waiting` response with no current room. Repeated responses may describe the same gameplay state; neither this count nor the requested tracker frequency proves distinct polls, sustained update frequency or atomic publication during every load transition. The first waiting response is retained, not counted as a gameplay match.

| Process | Observation | Loaded case | Current source / area:region | Source / region visits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `initial-item` | `item-before` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 1 / 1 |
| 1 | `item-later` | `item-after` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 1 / 1 |
| 2 | `item-later-restarted` | `item-after` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 1 / 1 |
| 2 | `item-earlier-restarted` | `item-before` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 1 / 1 |
| 2 | `door-earlier` | `door-before` | `01-04` / `re2-02:04` | 2 / 2 |
| 2 | `door-later` | `door-after` | `01-04` / `re2-02:04` | 2 / 2 |
| 3 | `door-earlier-restarted` | `door-before` | `01-04` / `re2-02:04` | 2 / 2 |
| 3 | `floor-upper` | `upper` | `00-16` / `re2-03:0D` | 3 / 3 |
| 3 | `floor-lower` | `lower` | `00-16` / `re2-02:11` | 3 / 4 |
| 4 | `floor-lower-restarted` | `lower` | `00-16` / `re2-02:11` | 3 / 4 |
| 4 | `floor-upper-restarted` | `upper` | `00-16` / `re2-03:0D` | 3 / 4 |
| 5 | `item-earlier-after-floors` | `item-before` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 3 / 4 |
| 5 | `item-later-after-floors` | `item-after` | `00-01` / `re2-00:01` | 3 / 4 |
| 5 | `door-later-after-floors` | `door-after` | `01-04` / `re2-02:04` | 3 / 4 |

The item case is the source-proven reward in `00-01`. Before collection, the published item is Uncollected and the room Incomplete; after collection it is Taken/Cleared. Both states recur after tracker restarts. Its departed-room publication also rebuilds from the loaded flags when another source is current. Earlier door/floor checkpoints predate that reward's collection, so they correctly return the already-explored item room to Incomplete.

The named door is `01-04:6430`. The pre-use state publishes Locked with its key symbol; the later state publishes Open/Unlocked without that symbol. Process 3 starts after the earlier state was loaded while the tracker was stopped and correctly reconstructs Locked. In this pass, departed observations remain Unchecked with the key symbol because those loaded checkpoints have the unlock bit clear. Unlocked departed labeling was separately verified in RE2-DOOR-002; this pass does not repeat that case. Observed access is never inferred from persisted exploration.

The floor cases retain source `00-16`. Upper selects `re2-03:0D`, lower selects `re2-02:11`. The upper region appears on its first accepted publication, and the lower appears only after its own first accepted publication. Process 4 starts on the lower context with both region visits intact, then an actual earlier load switches the current selection to upper. Process 5 starts after loading the earlier item state while the tracker was stopped; both floor regions and the door room remain explored. Later item/door loads again change their game-derived states without changing those visits.

Every settled sample's `explored_areas` agrees exactly with the history's entered regions. The final history contains exactly sources `00-01`, `01-04`, `00-16` and regions `re2-00:01`, `re2-02:04`, `re2-03:0D`, `re2-02:11`. Its schema has only version, profile/revision/scenario identity, one unchanged playthrough UUID, source visits and region visits. Items, completion, doors, current location and player pose are absent. Decoded history remains identical between stages with unchanged visits; the audit hashes a consistent reserialization of each captured history document rather than historical file bytes. No new room source extends the prior twenty-seven-source runtime union.

### Evidence, reproduction and limits

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-restart-20261019/`. `setup.py` verifies the isolated emulator/settings and boots `item-before`; `runner.py` starts/stops actual tracker processes, prepares selected slots after checking both achievement settings, and captures HTTP/history snapshots. Native F1 performs each of the eleven loads. `events.jsonl`, `run-1` through `run-5` logs/stdout, each observation's JSON and `.samples.json`, `author-metadata.json`, `black-check.json`, `cleanup.json` and `audit.json` preserve the pass. `audit.py` checks distinct process IDs, successful exits, checkpoint hashes, exact accumulated visits, identity/schema, every settled sample, current/departed item/door results, floor selection and unchanged-history groups. It checks all five logs for successful connection/resume/shutdown and absence of warning/error entries.

Use these pre-existing private savestates; their original preparation and assistance are recorded in the linked claims above. Checkpoint hashes identify the files loaded here, not new byte-identical full-RAM captures.

For independent expected item/door results, the audit reads the earlier named claims' unchanged private RAM captures: `rewind-before-activation`, `pickup-confirmed`, `key-present-locked-ready`, `key-use-complete`, `native-upper-approach` and `lower-later`. Their hashes are retained in `audit.json`. These reference captures are not newly observed RAM images. Acquisition ID 15 is a PS1 virtual unsigned 32-bit little-endian word at `0x800CC9E8`, mask `0x00010000`. The named door's bit 5 uses the same representation at `0x800CCB60`, mask `0x04000000`. Floor is an unsigned byte at PS1 virtual `0x800C7E2A`, mask `0xFF`. This pass adds no new address interpretation.

Before boot and every selected-slot load, Cheevos and Achievements were disabled. Memory cards were None, audio muted, per-game overrides absent and the existing black shader hash-checked. A private suppressed screenshot's central half was entirely black. No game image was emitted. Agent history/log paths were explicit; the user's history was not opened. Checkpoints retain their earlier high-health, infinite-magazine and position/floor preparation assistance. This pass sends no debugger RAM writes or new health/ammunition/position/floor/acquisition/key/inventory/story patches. It uses native savestate loads, not new pickup, unlock or movement interactions.

After testing, all five tracker processes and the isolated emulator were stopped. Settings and the selected slot were restored byte for byte, SHA256 `792443989dc8f5f8ee3dabcc2a20dd71a3e61cc0a44de8a84266d51e6e889069` and `995bb0ff5df6eb6b254d87ea9c6af5386105a50bfd50cdab018da50465965bfa`. Raw RAM, owned game data and savestates remain outside Git.

The added `test_cli_restarts_rebuild_item_and_door_state` uses synthetic memory and four production CLI initializations with the same disk history. It checks acquisition/completion and named-key access reversal, preserved identity/visits and exclusion of derived state from persistence. It does not launch a subprocess or emulator. An initial test setup incorrectly retained active item descriptors while setting acquired bits; the reader rejected that incoherent combination. The corrected setup removes/restores registry entries with the explicit synthetic acquisition state. This was a test-data error, not a production mismatch. The actual five-process pass is independent of that regression.

No UI changed, so the README screenshot remains unchanged. Other item/door/floor contexts, uninterrupted ordinary navigation, crash/power-loss recovery, memory-card persistence and user-history migration are outside this confirmation. Test/publication counts do not measure campaign readiness.

## RE2-MAP-002: door markers follow native trigger floors

Status: Confirmed for the listed two door pairs' source bindings, native crossing floors and the wrong-floor marker defect on SLUS-00421. The shared correction also removes ten other floor-incompatible placements offline. Their individual gameplay behavior and absolute visual wall alignment remain Candidate. The four retained tested segments are geometrically attached to contour walls; this does not independently establish their visual alignment with the original game.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, the same isolated bundle used by the linked captures. Runtime signature SHA256 remains `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) pins identity. This extends [RE2-MAP-001](#re2-map-001-native-leon-floor-selectors-and-numeric-contours), [RE2-DOOR-003](#re2-door-003-one-sided-unlock-and-savestate-reversal) and [RE2-FLOOR-001](#re2-floor-001-native-same-room-floor-changes-and-restores) without changing their original evidence.

### Defect and pinned native contract

Baseline `70792e5` projects every candidate door center onto every outlined region associated with its source, then chooses the nearest unambiguous wall within eight map units. This places `00-16:2522` on both `re2-02:11` and `re2-03:0D`. Its activation floor is zero, while the latter region is selected on floor four. That upper marker incorrectly refers to the lower doorway. The other side's destination-floor operand cannot establish this trigger's floor.

Door packet file offsets are zero-based RDT offsets. Both ordinary `0x3B` and four-point `0x68` packets store the source activation floor at opcode + 4. The registry pointer addresses opcode + 2, so the same byte is at descriptor + 2. The destination floor remains opcode + 25 or + 33 respectively. These are unsigned bytes, full mask `0xFF`, and are separate fields. Biohazard-utils' pinned [door decoder](https://github.com/biorand/biohazard-utils/blob/5d0d523cc0e228b5c9eea269149853cda5f266bd/src/IntelOrca.Biohazard/Script/Opcodes/DoorAotSeOpcode.cs) supplies a format lead; the owned pinned packets and executable independently establish the values used here.

In the pinned executable at PS1 virtual `0x8004DC2C..0x8004DC4C`, the native interaction matcher reads descriptor + 2, tests mask `0x80`, and bypasses equality when that bit is set. Otherwise it compares the full byte with actor + `0x106` and rejects a mismatch. For the player, that is the existing unsigned floor byte at PS1 virtual `0x800C7E2A`, width one, mask `0xFF`. Native branch instruction words are 32-bit little-endian, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`; executable offsets are `0x800 + address - 0x80010000`. Eight bounded Unicorn cases verify literal matches, mismatches and wildcard values 128, 132 and 255. This is offline execution of the pinned branch, not eight gameplay situations. `native-floor.txt`, `floor-xrefs.txt` and `native-floor-cases.json` retain the inspected range and numeric outcomes privately.

The decoder now records required `activation_floor` metadata for every door, leaving existing destination `floor` fields unchanged. Profile loading rejects missing, non-integer, boolean and out-of-byte-range activation floors. During generation, a non-wildcard trigger can belong only to native selector alternatives whose source, area, region and floor interval match that activation floor. The high wildcard bit preserves all candidate regions for that trigger. Existing selector conditions, projection, nearest-wall threshold, corner ambiguity and wall-segment rounding stay unchanged.

### Native crossings and restores

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-markers-20261019/`. The pass starts with the prior `primary-allowed-unlocked.sav`, SHA256 `d53939aa01ef1e52d9bfc25c6503dac35740ec77626291b8c5cd8160bedd304d`. Native repeated Cross enters `00-16` on floor 4. Native turning and repeated Cross return through its upper doorway. No coordinate/facing/floor write is made.

A native F1 load restores RE2-FLOOR-001's `native-upper-approach.sav`, SHA256 `ae5ba79981c64fb0a997713c1a1945c4aebbea1bdd1c8db7e6ac22b22b9d8450`. Native Up/Cross descends to floor 1. Turning toward negative Z and holding Up alone reaches the second stair approach but remains on floor 1; holding Up/Cross completes that descent to floor 0. Native turning/walking then reaches the lower door and repeated Cross enters `01-0b`; turning/repeated Cross returns to `00-16` on floor 0. These are two native door pairs, four crossings, not new item collection or unlocking operations.

| Source binding | Native floor | Destination | Published area:region | Retained segment |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `00-09:8432` | 0 | `00-16`, floor 4 | `re2-03:01` | X 240, Y 159.756 to 157.756 |
| `00-16:2554` | 4 | `00-09`, floor 0 | `re2-03:0D` | X 248, Y 159.524 to 157.524 |
| `00-16:2522` | 0 | `01-0b`, floor 0 | `re2-02:11` | X 251, Y 164.598 to 162.598 |
| `01-0b:12160` | 0 | `00-16`, floor 0 | `re2-02:0B` | X 251, Y 161.913 to 163.913 |

The following captures are newly observed in this pass. Bounded captures hash the consumed-read transcript; full captures hash 2 MiB RAM. All twelve publications replay unchanged through the corrected profile/reader, preserving three source visits and four region visits.

After the lower return, native F2 saves `lower-ready.sav`, SHA256 `d4888366bfdc864361e160ea199c33ff2e703f4860deafb8490d220fa07e5888`. Two more native F1 loads restore the earlier allowed-side state and this lower return. The new lower restore reproduces `lower-return` RAM byte for byte. The earlier allowed-side restore has the expected source/floor/pose but is not claimed byte-identical to the initial unpaused baseline. Three native F1 loads occur in total, including the upper stair approach. The private controller's tracker retains all entered regions throughout; this is not an additional actual CLI restart test or normal memory-card persistence test.

Reproduction timings are recorded to aid a repeat, not guarantee exact motion: initial O 2500 ms enters upper; Right 940 ms and O 2500 ms return. After the upper-approach restore, B 3300 ms descends to floor 1; Right 470 ms and Up 1600 ms reach the blocked second approach; B 2100 ms reaches floor 0. Right 470 ms and Up 2300 ms prepare the lower approach, then Up 800 ms and O 2500 ms enter its opposite side. Right 700 ms and O 2500 ms return. O repeats Cross at frequency five; B is the isolated Up/Cross macro at frequency zero. Paused observations separate the stages, with explicit GDB resume before movement and settled source checks before accepting crossings.

## RE2-COLLECT-004: exclusive event delivery collection flags

Status: Confirmed for the observed delivery, departed-room and restore contexts on SLUS-00421. The generic binding proof remains Candidate outside this one award identity. Conditional presence before delivery and ordinary reentry after this event remain unresolved. Repeating the same checkpoint does not validate a second identity or scenario.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`; runtime signature SHA256 `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) pins the revision. This extends the acquisition/activation distinction in [RE2-COLLECT-003](#re2-collect-003-scripted-activation-preserves-collection-identity), without changing its ordinary-pickup evidence.

### Defect, source and native delivery

Baseline `f0a957b` appends one permanently Unknown generic event item whenever a source has a relevant direct award. It does not consult a collection rule, even after the game records delivery. Source `01-02`, owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM2020.RDT`, SHA256 `6ee69fee262b4390ac5f4ecf36c54335cd7f6fa1b3b14af71720925bcb54e8a9`, has the catalog's one direct award. Procedure `17:2` contains opcode `0x76` at RDT file offset 8944, item type 53, quantity one. Its next command at 8947 is opcode `0x22`, bank 8, bit 5, operation one. These offsets are zero-based file offsets, not memory addresses. Each operand here is one unsigned byte, mask `0xFF`.

The pinned dispatch table at PS1 virtual `0x8009F94C` sends opcode `0x76` to `0x8005479C`. Its handler calls `0x800652BC` at `0x80054848`, then advances the script pointer by three. The ordinary-width inventory path calls `0x80064C70`, which stores type, quantity and slot metadata in four-byte slots. Inspection is retained in `native-award.txt`, `native-award-tail.txt` and `native-inventory.txt` privately. This inspection does not validate every award type, full-inventory behavior or every possible native inventory write.

The acquisition word is PS1 virtual `0x800CC9E8`, width four, unsigned little-endian. Bank 8 bit 5 uses mask `0x04000000`, with set meaning collected. This is the existing bank formula `0x80000000 >> (bit % 32)`, with four bytes per word. A debugger write watchpoint on that word stops twice after the native set operation, at PC `0x80050D08`. Each accepted stop has word `0xC4000000`, compared with `0xC0000000` before delivery, and inventory slot 3 changes from zero to bytes `35 01 00 00`. The slot is PS1 virtual `0x800CCB74`, width four; type and amount are the first two unsigned bytes, each mask `0xFF`. The private observation window starts at `0x800CCB68` and spans 44 bytes; that window is not an assertion of inventory capacity.

The two native deliveries repeat the same event from an earlier checkpoint. The first watch stop in the repeat instead reports PC `0x80081AA4` and word `0xC0000000`, with no delivered item. It is recorded as a non-delivery write, then execution resumes until the accepted set stop. `watch-observations.json` preserves the distinction. No watchpoint operation writes guest RAM.

Startup schedules this event under bank 1 bit 1 clear and bank 4 bit 7 clear. The latter becomes set near the sequence's end, after delivery. Bank 1 bit 1 is in PS1 virtual `0x800C7D04`, width four, unsigned little-endian, mask `0x40000000`; bank 4 bit 7 is in `0x800CC980`, same width/order, mask `0x01000000`. These activation/story gates are not acquisition rules. This increment deliberately does not turn them into a conditional presence proof. An unset collection bit therefore leaves this event item Unknown, and a known remaining ordinary item can still make the room Incomplete.

### Shared interpretation and conservative boundaries

Generation binds a direct award only to an immediately following set operation in that source's configured ordinary acquisition bank. A branch or procedure entry into the flag write bypassing the award prevents binding. An ordinary item or decoded item-interaction reset sharing that bank/bit also prevents binding. This includes a reset introducing an item absent from the creator catalog. The generator scans every supplied raw RDT for the three-byte flag-write prefix, including bytes that bounded decoding did not reach. The prefix must occur exactly once, at the adjacent writer. False matches conservatively leave the award unresolved. All source IDs are required at this boundary. The pinned 125-source corpus has exactly one such prefix for this rule, at 8947, and no ordinary-pickup alias. This rejects potential conflicting writers; it does not prove arbitrary unsupported script semantics. Undecoded pickup creators remain outside that alias proof. An exploratory scan for pickup-shaped bytes at every raw position produced false matches inside other commands and non-script data. It was rejected rather than used to reinterpret those bytes as real pickup declarations. The narrower decoded creator/reset check and raw flag-write check preserve the validated binding.

Generated awards require an explicit `collection` tag of `unresolved` or `flag`. Profile loading rejects malformed tags, wrong banks, boolean/non-integer/out-of-range fields, duplicate offsets and acquisition identities shared with another award or ordinary pickup. No room name, source ID, item type or bit number is hardcoded into the interpreter.

The bounded reader evaluates a bound acquisition flag from fresh state for every explored source. A set bit publishes the established item label, quantity and Taken state, including after inventory removal. A clear bit or unresolved binding retains a generic Unknown event check. Separate awards get separate checks. Unknown checks still prevent clearance; incomplete source coverage still prevents clearance after collection. Only the event check changes in the real captures. The source's other item gaps continue to yield Incomplete while current or Unknown after departure. No observed room is newly claimed Cleared.

These flag reads use the existing initial player/save blocks and consumed-byte coherence checks. A changing or failed final event-flag read cannot mutate tracker history. No full-RAM scan was added to polling, no collection verdict is persisted and no unvisited room is revealed.

### Native observations and restore checks

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-event-award-20261019/`. Boot uses the earlier `allowed-side-locked.sav`, SHA256 `ebbe528aa5b3021b9bd10155ccd101a9e21fb7153addcda2cb1f02099a89cf50`, from the rejected preliminary case documented in [RE2-DOOR-003](#re2-door-003-one-sided-unlock-and-savestate-reversal). Its failed unlock evidence remains rejected; this pass observes the delivery separately. Repeated native Cross advances the sequence while the word is watched. The native event then transfers the player to `01-00`.

## RE2-COLLECT-005: source-proven pre-delivery event presence

Status: Confirmed for required-item publication before the observed delivery, inactive earlier-state contexts, acquisition priority and the listed DuckStation restores on SLUS-00421. The source proof remains Candidate outside this one award identity. False-gate/no-acquisition gameplay cases and ordinary post-delivery reentry remain unvalidated. A failed sufficient condition means Unknown, never absence.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`; runtime signature SHA256 `712628cd70ecee75e834248085df57ac0b22862fa8e7e486e06cc9833cae5d5a`. [RE2-IDENTITY-001](#re2-identity-001-leon-a-disc-identity-and-candidate-room-layout) pins identity. [RE2-COLLECT-004](#re2-collect-004-exclusive-event-delivery-collection-flags) retains the source identity, native acquisition contract and initial delivery evidence.

### Defect and source proof

Baseline `be13a06` recognizes the direct event item only after collection. An unset acquisition bit leaves a generic Unknown check. In the departed earlier states below, ordinary item conditions are also unresolved, so the explored source remains Unknown even though its future delivery can be established from the source and current scenario/story flags.

Owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM2020.RDT`, source `01-02`, SHA256 `6ee69fee262b4390ac5f4ecf36c54335cd7f6fa1b3b14af71720925bcb54e8a9`, contains one bound direct award, type 53, quantity one, at zero-based RDT file offset 8944 in procedure `17:2`. Procedure `17:0` schedules that task at 8074 under nested bank 1 bit 1 clear and bank 4 bit 7 clear checks at 8060 and 8068. Other startup branches converge before scheduling and do not change its necessity. The sufficient condition is therefore `(bank 1 bit 1 == 0) AND (bank 4 bit 7 == 0)`. The first condition is already part of the profile's Leon A scenario mask. No room, item, procedure or flag identity is hardcoded into the presence interpreter.

Bank 1 bit 1 is PS1 virtual `0x800C7D04`, width four, unsigned little-endian, mask `0x40000000`. Bank 4 bit 7 is PS1 virtual `0x800CC980`, same width/order, mask `0x01000000`. Acquisition remains bank 8 bit 5 at PS1 virtual `0x800CC9E8`, width four, unsigned little-endian, mask `0x04000000`, set meaning collected. The script writes acquisition immediately after delivery, then sets the story gate near the sequence's end. A second writer in source `01-00` sets the story gate under another condition. Consequently that story bit alone is not an acquisition or absence rule.

Generation now proves both synchronous startup roots finish through supported effects, including after a fork. It rejects startup flag writes, unmodeled task control, unsupported loops and post-fork transitions. A source containing task cancellation/chaining or noncanonical/fixed-slot forks is withheld entirely. These checks prevent a queued-but-cancelled delivery or a changed post-startup flag from creating a false requirement. It then proves every supported continuation of the scheduled task reaches the bound award. Unknown actor comparisons and compound flag predicates require both outcomes; only single native flag predicates become exported conditions. Compound predicate joins need their own native proof and are not guessed.

A bounded Boolean formula records sufficient flag conditions, removes contradictory/subsumed clauses and combines complementary alternatives. Limits are 32 clauses, 32 predicates per clause, 4 call frames, 256 active analysis frames and 4096 visited analysis steps per proof. Exceeding a bound withholds the proof. Explicit metadata tags distinguish unresolved presence, unconditional requirement and conditional requirement. There is no implicit missing-field fallback. Profile loading validates condition shape, bank/bit/value types and bounds, rejects duplicate or contradictory predicates, and requires a bound acquisition identity before accepting a presence proof.

The synchronous initializer opcode set is shared with the existing ordinary-pickup proof. Its behavior and the seventeen ordinary item identities remain unchanged. Only this award gains a presence rule; all other room metadata and all floor metadata remain equal to the baseline.

### Native wait and continuation contract

The delivery calls a small do/wait/check procedure before the award. Its body is exactly the supported do, next-frame yield, padding, end-do, one flag check and end sequence. Suspension can wait for user input; it does not discard the caller's future item. Unknown loop forms remain unsupported.

Pinned handlers are at PS1 virtual `0x800506EC` for Do, `0x800500FC` for Evt_next, `0x80050760` for Edwhile, `0x80050A44` for Gosub and `0x80050AAC` for Return. The dispatch table is PS1 virtual `0x8009F94C`, entries four-byte little-endian executable addresses. Native opcode inspection is retained privately in `native-op-*.txt`. The cached, pinned [opcode enum](https://github.com/biorand/biohazard-utils/blob/5d0d523cc0e228b5c9eea269149853cda5f266bd/src/IntelOrca.Biohazard/Script/OpcodeV2.cs) and [decompiler](https://github.com/biorand/biohazard-utils/blob/5d0d523cc0e228b5c9eea269149853cda5f266bd/src/IntelOrca.Biohazard/Script/ScriptDecompiler.cs) supply format leads, not runtime confirmation.

Two bounded Unicorn cases execute the pinned Gosub, Do, yield, end-do and end handlers on a private RAM copy with a synthetic task/script. With the wait predicate true, execution returns to the loop body. With it false, execution exits and returns to the caller's award instruction; the subroutine level returns to zero. The save block stays unchanged in both cases. The tested predicate is bank 5 bit 33, PS1 virtual `0x800CC9A4`, width four, unsigned little-endian, mask `0x40000000`, compared with expected zero. `native_wait.py` and `native-wait-cases.json` retain the harness and results. Register/code addresses are PS1 virtual; Unicorn host reads/writes use physical RAM offsets. The first harness attempt incorrectly used virtual addresses with the host memory API and failed before execution; the corrected translation supplies the stated results. These private copy mutations are not live guest-memory patches or additional gameplay observations.

### Live observations, restores and rejected transitions

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-event-presence-20261019/`. The isolated instance boots the previous `allowed-side-locked.sav`, SHA256 `ebbe528aa5b3021b9bd10155ccd101a9e21fb7153addcda2cb1f02099a89cf50`. Its native event has already started by the first settled current-room observation. The initial artifact was renamed `active-initial` to reflect that fact. It is pre-delivery evidence, not a first-entry-before-activation observation.

After an earlier native F1 restore, repeated native Cross advances the same event. A write watch on the acquisition word stops at PC `0x80050D08` with word `0xC4000000`, compared with `0xC0000000` before delivery. The item changes Uncollected to Taken. The later native event transfers the player to `01-00`; the departed item's state remains Taken. Only one native delivery of one identity is counted in this pass.

The earlier `entrance-ready.sav`, SHA256 `156a026d3bce5b039b48f0f5f3a14f78a551e8b3be10ce5afcb4c0e1d0ce0c73`, restores source `01-00` with its event-stop/status markers clear, acquisition clear and the sufficient presence gates satisfied. The older `ammo-both.sav`, SHA256 `89e6315c5c8138784c9a27b138e90c78d4520107d1467b2f6b79d8f7baa7e570`, repeats that outcome with source `00-01` current and different story state. Neither case needs an active delivery sequence or cached item list.

The event-stop observation is bank 2 bit 7 at PS1 virtual `0x800C7D08`, width four, unsigned little-endian, mask `0x01000000`. Its clear value alone is not used as proof of presence or task identity. Scenario, source and acquisition/presence conditions establish the published item; the checkpoint provenance and additional status/current-source observations distinguish the inactive earlier contexts.
