The existing native audit and 390-test pass are shared with RE2-COLLECT-009; this source-only contribution adds no native interaction, production change or external code review. Local documentation/whitespace checks passed and unrelated worktree bytes were preserved.

## RE2-COLLECT-011: pinned native mode excludes an alternate pickup

Status: Confirmed for the stated SLUS-00421 bit-set profile context, two native initializations, three real pickups, repeat first collection, full clearance, departure/reentry and the two earlier/later restore pairs below. The opposite mode is explicitly unsupported. No global meaning or exhaustive lifetime proof for the flag is claimed.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`; DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Baseline commit `48b684c`. The human authorized this named mode-dependent exclusion increment for the current Leon A setup, with unproven cases retaining uncertainty. Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-mode-20261022/`.

### Native initialization and profile contract

A fresh owned-disc boot starts at PS1 BIOS virtual PC `0xBFC00000`, with the system word zero. A read-only execution breakpoint then stops at PS1 virtual `0x8002A944`, immediately before the initializer's store. The system word is still zero and register V0 contains `0x40`. Native Restart Game reproduces that same zero-word, same-PC, same-V0 observation. Both breakpoints are removed before resuming. The following two startup captures contain the set bit and correctly return NonGameplay without recording a visit. `startup-writer-first.json`, `startup-writer-repeat.json`, `startup-set.ram` and `startup-repeat.ram` retain these observations. Their RAM SHA256 values are `593553a21dfcbea537d1af41f3f0e44f2db847d7e2f2af1d95b22ee2c8af2173` and `ee5557b0282e9dd60bacf30f65e5c3c809b80d35ce2fd3277f063cf692783a69`.

The mode predicate is PS1 virtual `0x800C7CE4`, unsigned little-endian width four, mask `0x00000040`, required value `0x00000040`. It corresponds to script bank 0 bit 25. The separate Leon A scenario predicate remains at `0x800C7D04`, same width/order, mask `0xC2000000`, required value zero. The flag's in-game setting name is not inferred. The bounded exact-displacement scan finds nine direct-offset store leads; the shown local masks preserve this bit except the initializer that sets it. An earlier string scan also matched a negative displacement and is rejected. This scan does not cover every indirect native writer and cannot by itself establish lifetime stability.

The profile now requires explicit `system_mode_mask` and `system_mode_value`. Generation and profile loading share `scenario_flags` for both scenario and mode predicates; generated `fixed_scenario_flags` must match that complete context. Missing, boolean, zero-mask, oversized, unmasked and mismatched mode configuration fails during loading. Gameplay with another mode value returns WrongGame before item interpretation and changes no visits or publication. Changing-room Retry takes precedence over that rejection. Final coherence includes the mode mask independently of the quantity-override mask. This enforces the supported setup instead of silently extending a bit-set proof to another context. Mode-bit-clear gameplay and histories produced under an unsupported setup are outside this increment's acceptance evidence.

## RE2-QUANTITY-001: native pickup award amount differs from catalog

Status: Confirmed discrepancy for acquisition ID 141 in `01-08` on SLUS-00421, observed in both repeated-confirmation and isolated single-pickup sequences. The shared correction below matches the existing native captures and pinned handler. Mode-bit-clear behavior is Candidate with isolated native execution and synthetic regressions only. This correction adds no gameplay coverage.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`; DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Production commit `2f8978c`. [RE2-COLLECT-008's second native follow-up](#second-native-follow-up-2026-10-22) records setup, assistance, source identity, restore checks and cleanup. Private evidence shares `/tmp/re-map-re2-scripted-runtime-followup-20261022/`.

The owned creator at RDT file offset 4986 declares type 30, amount three, acquisition ID 141. Before interaction the live descriptor also says three and inventory contains no type 30. Opening a single native pickup prompt changes the descriptor amount to two before acquisition. One further confirmation sets acquisition and adds two units to inventory. Triangle closes the pickup display without another world interaction. Once settled, both the baseline and current tracker publish Taken with quantity three. An earlier repeated-Cross collection independently produced the same two-unit inventory addition. The separate ordinary ammunition pickup adds the expected fifteen units. This finding concerns the amount displayed; acquisition reversal and incomplete-room behavior still pass.

| Capture | Acquisition / inventory | Published result | Full RAM SHA256 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `second-target-before` | clear / zero | Available, three | `74ee52b6c8c6939f157dbceea4689afd5b2119194949e87f03266ca1a3fc5e18` |
| `quantity-single-prompt` | clear / zero; native descriptor two | Retry, previous publication retained | `e47c6ca94d81cecb2b2c384aebcc30caba686bcc3a3837f1acc370683418bf35` |
| `quantity-single-after` | set / two | Retry, previous publication retained | `179f893b53f59d612a5cce53ccd32dc1f7d78eaa9303a56c9b35aeedc89eef1f` |
| `quantity-single-settled` | set / two | Taken, three | `72b43f6d501a8ace69f4f9e99c69bca31ae0193c444cc08ef91ba71d4fe3a2ba` |

The source-bound loaded-RDT pointer is at PS1 virtual `0x800C6494`, unsigned little-endian width four, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. The descriptor amount is an unsigned little-endian halfword at loaded RDT base + 5002, file creator + 16, mask `0xFFFF`. Inventory type and amount at `0x800CCB70`/`0x800CCB71` are unsigned bytes, masks `0xFF`. Acquisition is the unsigned little-endian word at `0x800CC9F8`, mask `0x00040000`. These are PS1 virtual addresses/relative loaded-source offsets, not host-process addresses.

Reproduce from the private earlier checkpoint recorded in RE2-COLLECT-008. Prepare X/Z -19094/-16920, yaw 1024 on the native floor zero, then send one Cross press and a neutral interval. Pause and capture the prompt before any acquisition. Resume, confirm once, allow the display to settle, then cancel the pickup display and capture. Do not substitute an inventory edit or repeated world interactions for this isolated sequence. The observed adjustment already exists before collection, so later item consumption does not explain this case. No difficulty-dependent formula is confirmed yet.

The reader uses a live amount only for an available interaction. Once taken or departed, it selects the single generated amount when every catalog variant agrees. Here the catalog contains only three, so the amount returns to three even though the game grants two. This identifies the reader/catalog seam that needs a shared native award rule or explicit unreconstructed quantity. Retaining a transient pickup amount as cached history would not meet savestate/fresh-start requirements. No named-item exception or quantity fallback is introduced.

Contributions:

- 2026-10-22; Codex root, exact verified model `gpt-6.1-sol`; discovered the inventory/display difference during the approved exclusion test, reproduced it with discrete native inputs after an earlier restore, verified the prompt/acquisition/inventory bytes and unchanged Retry publications, inspected the existing quantity selection and authored this finding. The cause and correction have not been reviewed or implemented.


### Shared correction, 2026-10-22

The human user's continuation approves the separately named shared pickup-quantity correction. Private implementation and replay evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-quantity-20261022/`. Baseline is `7d0e072`; game/executable/emulator identities and original capture hashes remain as recorded above. This pass boots no emulator, changes no guest memory and opens only separate agent histories.

Pinned executable inspection identifies the ordinary AOT item handler at PS1 virtual `0x8004E330` through `0x8004E3E8`. Native dispatch at `0x80051858` through `0x800518B8` selects operand offset 12 or 20 from the interaction's four-point bit, then dispatches by interaction kind. The function-pointer table begins at `0x8009F87C`; kind two's unsigned little-endian four-byte entry at `0x8009F884`, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`, points to `0x8004E330`. The handler reads the unsigned little-endian system word at `0x800C7CE4`, width four, mask `0x00000040`. If this bit is set and the operand's one-byte item type is 30, it stores amount two at operand + 2, an unsigned little-endian halfword with mask `0xFFFF`, before opening the prompt. Otherwise it leaves the source amount unchanged. This assignment also replaces input zero or an already adjusted two. No difficulty label or cross-revision formula is inferred from the bit.

`native_check.py` executes the complete pinned handler in isolated Unicorn copies with all 104 non-file item types, both bit states and input amounts 0, 1, 2, 3, 15, 255, 256 and 65535. All 1,664 cases return normally and match that branch. `native-check.json` records the executable hash, table reference and each input/output. This is native instruction execution, not gameplay, and does not validate inventory stacking or capacity limits.

The revision profile now requires an explicit pickup-quantity override. Malformed or missing configuration fails at profile loading. The reader applies the same rule after choosing available live amounts or source quantity alternatives, so current, departed, collected and fresh-history observations agree without caching a transient amount. Under the active native override, conflicting source amounts collapse to the same two-unit award. Without it, conflicting alternatives remain `Unreconstructed`. An ambiguous item identity remains unresolved. Event deliveries retain their existing amounts. The pinned opcode `0x76` handler at `0x8005479C` through `0x80054878` reads the type/amount bytes from the script and calls inventory helper `0x800652BC`, bypassing the ordinary AOT handler. The system mode mask also participates in final coherence checks; a bit change rejects the whole observation without updating visits or publication.

`audit.py` replays the eleven captures from the first native exclusion follow-up and eighteen from the second follow-up through the baseline and corrected readers. All 26 Display and three Retry statuses agree. Every published field except the affected type 30 pickup quantities is identical, including collection, completion, room/floor/pose, visits and doors. The original `second-target-before` now predicts Available with quantity two before prompt entry; `quantity-single-settled` reports Taken with two, matching the recorded inventory addition. Native departure/reentry and earlier/later restored captures retain that amount. A combined private history stores eight existing source visits and eight region visits, with no item or completion fields. Four fresh reconstructions of earlier, later, departed-first and discrete-settled captures recover quantity two and unchanged Incomplete status. These are offline reads of previously captured gameplay, not new native pickups, loads or live tracker restarts. The recorded runtime-source union remains twenty-eight.

The second synthetic creator is source `00-0f`, acquisition ID 139, the earlier RE2-COLLECT-007 case. An initial test parameter incorrectly selected `01-0f`, which has no matching item and failed before a poll. Correcting the source ID makes both creator contexts exercise the shared rule; this failure adds no evidence. Initial scoped lint/format findings were confined to the new test and corrected. The focused RE2/profile/history/logging suite then passed 390 tests. Scoped Ruff lint/format and all 133 RE2 browser outlines passed.

## RE2-COLLECT-012: counter initialization blocks departed item presence

Initial proposal status, 2026-10-23: Candidate shared presence correction for SLUS-00421. Exact native instruction execution and replay of existing captures establish the bounded lead below. No production proof, metadata or behavior changes are implemented. No new gameplay, restore or runtime-source coverage is claimed. Baseline `7d46f96`; private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-counter-presence-20261023/`. The dated implementation and native-validation follow-ups below retain this proposal's original limits separately from their later results.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`; reused captures were recorded on DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Owned source `PL0/RDT/ROOM1150.RDT`, source ID `00-15`, is 347152 bytes, SHA256 `ed5c71d21cdbf73c2e29ca33a23ea0c4bf09608217a1101e62f33ab4ccdba942`. [RE2-COLLECT-009](#re2-collect-009-native-pickup-and-use-keep-acquisition-identities-separate) records the native acquisition/reentry/restore observations and assistance for this source. This contribution reuses that evidence without treating its captured counter transitions as a new instruction watchpoint observation.

Main initializer `17:0` calls `17:12` at RDT offset 5458. That procedure initializes work counter seven to zero with opcode `0x24` at 6548, then creates item ID 209 at 6552. A second `0x24` in `17:14`, offset 6692, assigns the same counter value 51. The existing presence analysis does not interpret this opcode. It therefore exports neither unconditional collection IDs nor conditional presence for the room. This blocks proof of both ID 209 and the ordinary ID 101, whose alternative startup creators agree on identity. An initial audit assertion expecting only the zero assignment is rejected; both source packets are retained.

The pinned opcode table at PS1 virtual `0x8009F94C`, unsigned little-endian four-byte entries with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`, selects `0x80050DB8` for opcode `0x24`. That leaf reads the unsigned byte counter index, the signed little-endian source halfword, advances the selected task pointer by four and writes the low halfword to `0x800CC918 + 2 * index`. The stored value is unsigned little-endian width two, mask `0xFFFF`. Both actual source operands select PS1 virtual `0x800CC926`. The task instruction pointer remains a separate unsigned little-endian four-byte field at task offset `0x1C`, mask `0xFFFFFFFF`.

`native_save.py` runs the exact handler over all 256 byte indices with values zero, 51 and 65535 in isolated copied RAM. All 768 cases return one and produce exactly the task-pointer and selected halfword writes. The initial write-hook comparison of 65535 fails because Unicorn reports the sign-extended register value for `SH`. Comparing only the store width and independently reading the resulting two bytes corrects the probe; it does not change the native rule.

These cases also demonstrate why adding `0x24` to a blanket fall-through list is unsafe. Counter 52 addresses flag bank four at `0x800CC980`; counter 53 addresses its next halfword at `0x800CC982`. Counters 104 and 120 address acquisition banks eight and thirty-four at `0x800CC9E8` and `0x800CCA08`. All addresses here are PS1 virtual, not host addresses. Production handling must establish which state each write can affect and reject unsafe or unproved aliases. This audit does not establish a general safe counter-index range.

`candidate.py` makes a process-local hypothesis only for this exact source, after asserting that both `0x24` operands select counter seven. With that added assumption, the existing shared startup analysis derives required IDs `[101, 209]`. No named-item exception is added. The script then supplies only those hypothetical presence fields to a separate in-memory profile. Repository modules, profile and generated metadata stay unchanged.

For each profile, a fresh tracker first reads the existing native `activation-start.ram`, SHA256 `fa2f75014f7faa8680c3a575c257c40d164183755f2fbcba1d3f5343705ad068`, in `00-15`. It then reads the earlier native `target-entry.ram`, SHA256 `f08ad0ff27d6b2fb478787508619ecc8455dcd54e64af62cd050d38181d206bc`, in `01-09`. This combines two existing native captures to model an earlier restore after exploration; it is not a newly observed native load or ordinary departure sequence. The current-room item rows are identical under both profiles. In the second read, baseline ID 101 and ID 209 are Unknown. The hypothetical profile reconstructs both as Uncollected and the explored source changes from Unknown to Incomplete. Other item states, labels and quantities stay unchanged. Unresolved checks remain and full clearance is not claimed.

The proposed feature is shared presence analysis for safe counter initialization. Its scope is to model the native write explicitly, preserve uncertainty for unsafe aliases and unsupported effects, regenerate affected proofs and verify collection, departure/reentry and earlier/later reconstruction. Implementation approval is pending. The separate parked ID 159 case remains unresolved and receives no exclusion from this finding. The new source check also finds no decoded script-rewrite opcode `0x1B` in `ROOM2080.RDT` or the matched auxiliary script; this supplies no new activation route and does not settle physical membership.

Reproduce with `python3 /tmp/re-map-re2-counter-presence-20261023/candidate.py` and `PYTHONPATH=/tmp/re-map-re2-scripted-exclusions-20261022/deps python3 /tmp/re-map-re2-counter-presence-20261023/native_save.py`. `candidate.json` and `native-save.json` retain the hypothetical fields, capture identities, exact native cases and limits. Original owned data, executable bytes, raw RAM and saves remain private. No emulator is booted, no live memory or history is changed and the README image is unchanged.

Contributions:

- 2026-10-23; human user; requested continued work on the approved completion gap.
- 2026-10-23; Codex root, exact verified model `gpt-6.1-sol`; checked the remaining script-rewrite lead, identified the blocked startup proof, reproduced the departed-state difference using existing captures, executed the isolated native write/alias cases and authored this Candidate proposal. `author.json` verifies matching session/latest-turn metadata. The private probes, local documentation links, whitespace and preservation of unrelated RE1 work pass. This contribution claims no new runtime validation or external model review.

### Shared counter-write guard, 2026-10-23

## RE2-COLLECT-013: unrelated startup flag writes block conditional presence

Initial proposal status, 2026-10-23: Candidate shared presence correction for SLUS-00421, inspected against baseline `649517b`. This is a process-local hypothesis over owned source and existing captures. No production implementation, new native interaction, restore or runtime-source coverage is claimed. Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-flag-write-proposal-20261023/`. The dated implementation and native follow-up below record the later results separately.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM1150.RDT`, source `00-15`, is 347152 bytes, SHA256 `ed5c71d21cdbf73c2e29ca33a23ea0c4bf09608217a1101e62f33ab4ccdba942`. The reused captures are RE2-COLLECT-012's preceding native pass on DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Its current-room observations establish another available pickup, ID 40, which becomes Unknown after departure. Once IDs 101 and 209 are taken, departed completion is Unknown despite that remaining pickup.

The shared symbolic presence proof requires both startup roots to finish without unsupported effects. It currently rejects every opcode `0x22` flag write. Init root `16:0` contains five such writes at RDT file offsets 4866 through 4882, before its end. They affect bank 4 bit 15 and bank 8 bits 104, 20, 103 and 43. They do not change any configured presence-input bit under the pinned profile's flag-bank mapping. The probe checks all thirty decoded writers in the complete source, operations zero and one, against all four configured inputs. Each target stays within its configured bank and has no canonical address/mask overlap with an input. This source-bound comparison does not establish general native write semantics or safe handling of arbitrary bank/index aliases.

The isolated hypothesis permits these unrelated writes to pass the shared startup proof. It leaves the profile's stable-input set unchanged. The resulting sufficient presence conditions for IDs 40 and 104 both require bank 1 bit 1 clear. That input is PS1 virtual `0x800C7D04`, unsigned little-endian width four, mask `0x40000000`, required value zero, already part of the pinned scenario. Unconditional IDs 101/209 remain unchanged. ID 104 is already Taken in the reused observations, so its extra conditional proof changes no published item state.

Eleven existing captures are checked through fresh baseline and hypothetical trackers. Six departed observations change only ID 40 from Unknown to Uncollected. In `both-departed` and `later-departed-restored`, the shared completion policy consequently changes Unknown to Incomplete. The other four already have a known remaining item and remain Incomplete. All current-room item states, acquired counts, quantities, doors, pose, floor and visits compare equal. This is replay of earlier observations, not a new pickup or savestate load. The initial probe incorrectly indexed the decoder's item list by an RDT offset and stops before comparison; the corrected probe uses the offset-keyed catalog and passes all eleven captures.

The proposed feature is shared conditional presence through unrelated startup flag writes. A production implementation must bind decoded writes to audited native effects and canonical profile addresses, reject unsupported/out-of-range aliases and retain uncertainty for writes affecting presence inputs. Generation and loading must agree on the proof contract. The scoped validation should cover the identified pickup, departure/reentry, earlier/later reconstruction and unsafe-write counterexamples. No named-item exception or expanded stable-flag whitelist is proposed. The separate ID 103 conflicting-type check and parked ID 159 case remain unresolved; neither receives an exclusion or clearance rule. Implementation approval is pending.

Reproduce with `python3 /tmp/re-map-re2-flag-write-proposal-20261023/probe.py`. `candidate.json` retains exact decoded writers, configured inputs, hypothetical conditions, capture hashes and outcome comparisons. Repository modules are compiled into an isolated namespace for the experiment; production code, emulator state and exploration history remain unchanged. Raw RAM, owned assets and probe helpers remain private.

### Shared flag-write guard and native pickup, 2026-10-23

Status: Confirmed for ID 40 pickup, departed presence, repeat collection, successful ordinary departure/reentry and the six listed DuckStation restores on SLUS-00421. The shared guard is implemented against baseline `211fec7`. Four sources gain five source-derived conditional proofs; this pass does not confirm all their native lifecycles. Other unresolved items and full clearance retain their existing limits. Private implementation evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-flag-write-implementation-20261023/`; new gameplay evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-flag-write-native-20261023/`.

The game, owned `ROOM1150.RDT` and executable identities remain those pinned above. The isolated DuckStation remains `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Native opcode `0x22` selects handler PS1 virtual `0x80050C50` from the pinned dispatch table. Its bank and bit operands are unsigned bytes. The pointer table at PS1 virtual `0x8009FD38` contains unsigned little-endian four-byte pointers. The target word is `table[bank] + 4 * (bit // 32)`, unsigned little-endian width four, with mask `0x80000000 >> (bit % 32)`. Operations zero, one and seven clear, set and toggle that bit. The packet advances the task PC by four. The inspected leaf has no calls or other state writes before its shared JR/NOP epilogue.

`native_flags.py` executes all 256 bit operands for all thirty-six native table entries, three operations and three seeds, 82,944 copied-RAM cases. All configured bank pointers match both the pinned executable and captured table. Bank eleven and indices outside configured widths are explicit negative controls, not accepted proof inputs. Seventy-two additional cases use operations two and 255; they advance the task PC without a flag store. Production conservatively rejects these unsupported operation values. The audit checks every actual store, its width, target mask and resulting word. This is instruction execution over copied RAM, not new gameplay or native watchpoint confirmation of every accepted bit.

The first whole-leaf attempt reaches the expected stores and then raises a copied-emulation return exception. The bounded audit stops at the common JR/NOP epilogue `0x80050D08`, with the fall-through XOR path reporting its final store PC `0x80050D04`. It claims no executed return. Restoring a pristine Unicorn CPU context between cases prevents a previous stopped branch from affecting the next case. These probe corrections change no game data or production interpretation. `native-flags.json` retains the case count, deterministic result digest, native table and limits.

One shared validator requires an aligned pointer table within PS1 RAM, typed unique bank declarations, whole-word widths within the bounded player/save reads and an explicit stable-input list. It computes native word/mask aliases against every configured presence input. A decoded write is admitted only for a supported operation, a known bank, an index within its declared width and a disjoint canonical bit. Different bank numbers and offset aliases cannot hide a protected write; independent bits in the same word remain eligible. The existing scenario-exclusion proof reuses the same canonical alias calculation and operation constants, retaining its previous behavior.

Both ordinary conditional pickups and scheduled event presence use the decoded guard. Startup writes affecting a presence input remain unresolved, including after task queuing. No stable flag is added. The decoder requires an explicit write-proof set; all forty-five repository decoder calls migrate together, with no compatibility default. Generation verifies the configured table entries directly against the pinned executable before interpreting rooms. Loader and generator share the proof contract containing the table address, operations, banks and condition inputs. The loader compares typed JSON contracts so boolean operands cannot equal integer zero or one. Missing, malformed and mismatched contracts fail startup with diagnostics. Shifted-table and shifted-bank private generation controls both fail at native-table binding; an initial shifted system-bank control is rejected even earlier by the existing scenario validator and does not test that later guard.

| `00-10` | 17 | bank 1 bit 1 set and bit 6 clear |
| `00-15` | 40, 104 | bank 1 bit 1 clear |

Every other room field, exclusion, unconditional proof, quantity and floor-plan byte remains unchanged. The `00-10` predicate is incompatible with the pinned Leon A scenario. Its failed predicate establishes no absence, and a true predicate in another scenario cannot bypass WrongGame or record visits. The new tests cover this boundary with both acquisition values and both values of the second predicate. Source `00-15`'s conflicting ID 103 and the separate parked ID 159 remain unresolved. Existing persisted exploration and runtime item-state formats remain compatible; profile and generated metadata ship together.

## RE2-COLLECT-014: fixed scenario selects conflicting pickup identities

Status: Confirmed for the listed native collection, repeat collection, ordinary departure/reentry and savestate restores of all three selected identities on SLUS-00421. ID 103 and the [third case, ID 65](#third-selected-identity-native-lifecycle-2026-10-24), also have full-clearance observations. The [second case, ID 85](#second-selected-identity-native-lifecycle-2026-10-23), retains other unresolved pickups. ID 65's original departed-presence gap is subsequently resolved by [RE2-COLLECT-015](#re2-collect-015-counter-independent-pickup-presence); its original native collection and restore evidence remains valid. The original implementation starts at `6ae3d16`; the parked ID 159 check and campaign readiness remain unresolved.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`; isolated DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Owned `ROOM1150.RDT` remains 347152 bytes, SHA256 `ed5c71d21cdbf73c2e29ca33a23ea0c4bf09608217a1101e62f33ab4ccdba942`. Private evidence and reproducible helpers are under `/tmp/re-map-re2-scenario-identities-20261023/`, with new game observations under its `native/` directory. No raw RAM, checkpoint, source binary or game pixels are committed.

### Failure and shared proof

ID 103 has type 7/quantity five at zero-based RDT offset 5332 and type 5/quantity eight at 5396, both slot thirteen. The startup branch at 5242 checks bank 1 bit 1 at 5246. The enforced Leon A context selects the first creator. Bank 1 is PS1 virtual `0x800C7D04`, four-byte unsigned little-endian; bit 1 has mask `0x40000000`, within required scenario mask `0xC2000000`/value zero. The mode word is PS1 virtual `0x800C7CE4`, the same width/order, required mask/value `0x00000040`. At baseline, an active descriptor identifies it, but a missing/inactive descriptor leaves both catalog types possible. Acquisition alone cannot select between them, so departure or collection leaves an Unknown check that prevents clearance.

`scenario_item_proofs` shares the existing complete-source, fixed-context audit between whole-identity exclusion and creator-variant selection. Every procedure is independently considered, including uncalled procedures. Unknown predicates retain both paths; every decoded creator in an opaque wait loop remains possible. Resets introducing the acquisition ID prevent variant selection. Unsafe direct/aliased fixed-bit writes, unsupported effects, malformed control flow, incomplete catalogs and exhausted work bounds withhold the proof. Selection requires a conflicting raw identity and exactly one type among all possible creators. It does not establish presence.

Both raw and selected candidates use the same `collection_presence` implementation. After proven impossible variants are removed from that input, all existing slot replacement, reset, condition, counter, task and lifetime guards still apply. Generator, metadata loader and live reader share `scenario_item_candidates`. Raw definitions remain available for exact live descriptor binding. A live excluded creator returns Retry before visits or publication. Fresh acquisition flags determine Taken; fresh sufficient conditions determine Uncollected. No item state or variant selection is persisted with exploration.

Every source now requires an explicit `scenario_item_variants` list. Missing or malformed rows fail startup, including booleans, duplicate/empty/foreign offsets, mixed types, duplicate IDs, unambiguous identities and incomplete source claims. Selections bind to catalog offsets and the existing strict fixed-context contract. Profile and generated metadata ship together; exploration and other persisted formats are unchanged.

Generation selects `00-15` ID 103/type 7 at 5332, `01-10` ID 85/type 5 at 4824 and `04-08` ID 65/type 38 at 5520. The first two gain sufficient presence under bank 1 bit 1 clear. The third retains unresolved presence. All existing exclusions, required identities, other conditional rules, amounts, award rules and map bytes stay unchanged. `metadata-changes.json` records the bounded differences. Opposite scenario values are exercised only in source tests; the runtime Leon A gate is retained.

### Native effects needed by the complete-source audit

The first generation selects only `04-08`; the target source is rejected. Private `initial-proof-rejections.json` preserves diagnostic hypothetical substitutions, which are not accepted evidence or production interpretation. The actual blockers are actor attachment `0x48`, forced actor mode `0x66`, the input predicate `0x50`, a while/endwhile pair `0x0F`/`0x10` and five nested actor predicates. The full-source rule is extended only after inspecting the pinned native handlers and executing them over copied RAM.

All code/data addresses here are PS1 virtual unless identified as RDT offsets. Native instruction words and pointers are four-byte little-endian with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. The command table remains `0x8009F94C`. The `0x48` handler is `0x80051B8C..0x80051CF8`; its stores are the selected actor's pointer at offset `0x80`, signed position words at `0x38/0x3C/0x40`, signed halfwords at `0x74/0x76/0x78` and task PC. Halfwords have mask `0xFFFF`. It makes no calls. `0x66`, `0x80053670..0x800536B0`, saves the selected actor's four-byte word at offset four to the task word at `0x170` and replaces it with `0x1404`, or restores the saved word when already `0x1404`, then advances task PC. These use the same native selected actor as the existing audited actor effects.

`0x50`, `0x80053DD0..0x80053E04`, reads the input word at `0x800C6480`, applies an unsigned little-endian halfword mask from the packet, and stores only the four-byte task PC. Native while/endwhile handlers `0x800505D8..0x800506A0` and `0x800506A8..0x800506E4` write their task frame/PC state. The while evaluator is exercised with that read-only input predicate. Loop analysis requires paired local bounds and a single recognized read-only predicate. Whole loop procedures contribute every creator without fixed-predicate pruning. Mismatched or unbalanced loops and interior IF/Else control remain unsupported. The conditional analysis cap grows from four to eight to retain the source's five opaque checks; its 4,096-state work cap remains enforced. This is an analysis bound, not a claim about native stack capacity.

`native_effects.py` checks 1,068 copied native cases: 1,024 attachment operand/actor combinations, four forced-mode cases, sixteen input-predicate cases, sixteen while cases and eight endwhile cases. Every store is checked against the explicit copied task/stack/actor regions and fixed/acquisition words remain equal. Runs stop at the handlers' final JR after all stores; they do not claim an executed top-level return. The copied pointer tables and selected actors are explicit test inputs, not evidence that arbitrary malformed pointers are safe. No runtime patch, guest write or new gameplay is performed by this audit.

## RE2-SOURCE-001: bounded sound relocation preserves room identity

Status: Confirmed for the four recorded native observations in sources `03-04` and `03-05` on the named revision. Game: Resident Evil 2 USA, Disc 1 Leon A, SLUS-00421. Executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. It launches no emulator and claims no new gameplay or savestate loads.

Reproduction: read `alternate-connected-area-verified.ram`, `alternate-connected-area-settled.ram`, `alternate-forward-result.ram` and `movement-third-descent.ram` from `/tmp/re-map-re2-third-variant-native-20261023/native/`. At baseline `eeb35fe`, every capture returns Retry with `Loaded room section layout does not match its source`. Both settled free-control sources fail, so waiting for room loading cannot resolve the defect.

The loaded RDT base is the unsigned little-endian u32 pointer at PS1 virtual `0x800C6494`, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. It is `0x8011A480` in these captures. Section zero and one fields at `base + 8` and `base + 12` are also u32 little-endian, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. The remaining 21 fields occupy `base + 16` through `base + 96` at four-byte intervals. The [pinned RDT structure](https://github.com/OpenBiohazard2/OpenBiohazard2/blob/fd525d4b80539b933bea3146069204df6f8c0d0f/fileio/rdt.go#L25-L49) identifies the first pair as the EDT and VAB header; cameras use section seven. The owned executable's native sound loader at `0x80055D4C..0x80055DF0` reads the pair and passes the VAB header to the sound API. Static inspection does not identify the writer responsible for the captured displacement.

| Source | Source-relative EDT/VH offsets | Observed EDT/VH PS1 virtual pointers | Shared displacement |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `03-04` | `0xBEFC` / `0xBFBC` | `0x80121CE4` / `0x80121DA4` | `-0x4698` |
| `03-05` | `0xD6F4` / `0xD7B4` | `0x801244FC` / `0x801245BC` | `-0x3678` |

At each moved EDT pointer, all 192 bytes exactly match the owned room source. At each moved VH pointer, the first 32 bytes match. Hashing the entire 3,104-byte VH is rejected: 555 bytes differ in the two `03-04` captures and 528 in the two `03-05` captures, beginning at byte 40 in program records. Sample-bank contents and the old aliased EDT location also change, so neither whole-room hashing nor demanding the original sound location describes these observations. `sound-content.json` retains the full comparisons and stable-byte hashes.

The decoder now generates two required sound proofs per source, with section index, width and SHA256. The loader validates exact indices zero/one, widths 192/32, lowercase 64-digit hashes, source bounds and the catalog's 192-byte spacing. Missing or malformed proofs fail at startup. Generation from the owned CUE produces all 125 proof pairs; stripping only the new pairs and claim ID makes the room metadata identical to baseline. Generated floor plans are byte-identical.

The reader retains exact source-relative equality for sections two through 22. When the sound pair differs from its original offsets, both pointers must share one displacement, remain four-byte aligned and keep their entire hashed windows inside the loaded source after its 100-byte header. Both hashes must match. Every existing camera proof remains required. All 125 catalog identities remain unique using the other 21 section offsets and camera hashes alone. This is a catalog identity check, not new gameplay coverage.

The existing 92-byte header read stays in final coherence validation. Both moved sound windows also receive fresh coherence reads before publication. A mismatch returns Retry, a failed or short read returns ReadFailure, and neither changes exploration or current-room publication. The original layout keeps its previous checks and adds no sound-window reads. A moved layout adds 224 proof bytes and their 224 coherence bytes; it does not scan the bank or full RAM.

`/tmp/re-map-re2-sound-binding-20261024/` contains `plan.json`, `author.json`, owned-executable disassembly snippets, `sound-content.json`, `replay.py` and `capture-replay.json`. Replay compares complete trackers and rendered publications with baseline over 169 untouched native captures. All 165 previously accepted or conservatively rejected results remain identical. These copied bytes are an interpretation check, not a native game observation.

The previous 63-capture native pass also replays in original order with one retained exploration history and now publishes all 63 observations. The accepted-source union rises from 53 to 55 of 125. The two added entries establish source binding, not complete item/door lifecycles or campaign readiness. Existing Unknown item states, the parked pickup, other scenarios and normal memory-card persistence retain their limits.

## RE2-COLLECT-015: counter-independent pickup presence

Status: Confirmed native lifecycle observations for all six scenario-compatible acquisition IDs added by this proof on the named revision. These comprise the [recorded `04-08` ID 65 case](#third-selected-identity-native-lifecycle-2026-10-24), [source `04-08`, ID 64 with its neighbor collected first](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-04-08-id-64-2026-10-24), [source `00-13`, ID 18](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-00-13-2026-10-24), [source `05-09`, ID 6](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-05-09-2026-10-24), and [source `05-0e`, IDs 0 and 1 with native consumption](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-05-0e-2026-10-24). Native comparison effects are checked by isolated instruction execution over copied RAM. Seven generated proofs across four sources have source/regression validation; ID 30's predicate is incompatible with this profile. Unobserved conditions and newly executed startup-branch coverage retain their limits.

Game: Resident Evil 2 USA, Disc 1 Leon A, SLUS-00421. Executable `SLUS_004.21` SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Original captured gameplay uses isolated DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`. Baseline `19332fa` includes the sound-binding fix. Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-counter-presence-20261024/`; owned sources and original captures remain in their earlier private evidence directories.

### Reproduced failure and source cause

The preceding third selected-identity lifecycle records native pickup ID 65 as Available in `04-08`, Unknown after departure to `04-02`, and Taken after collection. Its neighbor ID 64 has the same lack of a source presence proof before collection. Thus `target-before-departed` and the identical earlier departed restores report Unknown despite a source-required remaining pickup. The revised interpretation reports Uncollected and Incomplete; collection remains Taken/Cleared. No acquisition rule or exclusion is changed.

Owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM5080.RDT`, 64,532 bytes, SHA256 `efbcb217a0c97ac1004a6a1753193d1da3828afd4d1f74d9a285bebfb7f794c1`, calls procedure `17:4` before declaring either pickup in `17:2`. The former includes an IF at RDT offset 5678 followed by `23 00 1B 00 02 05` at 5682. It compares counter 27 with signed value 1282 using operation zero. Its branches contain actor setup and return to the later pickup declarations. The shared presence interpreter previously recognized the actor predicate but rejected this counter predicate before it could prove either startup root finished.

A private hypothesis changes only that predicate admission in an isolated Python namespace. Its full-catalog result predicts the same seven rules as the actual generation. No hypothetical rule is deployed before native effect inspection. `candidate-changes.json`, `metadata-changes.json` and `probe-corrections.json` retain the predicted/actual scope and rejected harness comparisons.

### Native effect and proof contract

Addresses below are PS1 virtual. The command dispatch table at `0x8009F94C` contains unsigned u32 little-endian pointers, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`; entry `0x23` selects `0x80050D10`. The handler reads its task PC at `task + 0x1C`, the packet's unsigned byte counter index at offset two, unsigned byte operation at offset three, and signed little-endian halfword right operand at offset four. The counter is a signed little-endian halfword at `0x800CC918 + 2 * index`, raw mask `0xFFFF`. The observed index 27 therefore reads `0x800CC94E`. Packet byte one is ignored.

The seven-entry native jump table at `0x80010E78`, u32 little-endian pointers with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`, selects equality, greater-than, greater-or-equal, less-than, less-or-equal, inequality and nonzero bitwise AND for operators zero through six. Every path stores only `packet + 6` to the task PC, makes no calls, and reaches the common JR at `0x80050DB0`. Values seven and above skip that table and return an incidental index-derived value. They remain unsupported by the source proof.

`native-counter-comparison.txt` records pinned disassembly. `native_effects.py` checks 89,600 supported copied-native cases over all 256 byte indices, operations zero through six, ignored-padding values zero/255, and every pair of signed values -32768, -1, 0, 1 and 32767. All stores must be exactly the single four-byte PC advance. Both comparison outcomes occur for every operation. Another 512 cases check operators seven/255 across all indices; they do not broaden support. The audit checks captured code/table bytes against the owned executable and records a deterministic result digest in `native-effects.json`. Execution stops before the final JR, after all stores and result computation; no executed top-level return or fresh game observation is claimed.

The shared presence proof now admits those seven counter predicates where it already handles an opaque actor predicate. It proves both continuations and intersects their sufficient conditions. It never reads a live counter or exports one as a fresh presence condition. A pickup reached on only one counter outcome stays unresolved. Early returns obey their existing caller continuations. Unsupported operators, incomplete packets, compound predicates, startup transitions and unsafe writes retain the existing conservative result. Existing acquisition, identity/slot, task cancellation, work bounds, source completeness, mode/scenario and coherence checks are unchanged.

### Generated scope and recorded reconstruction

Generation adds only these presence rules:

| --- | --- | --- |
| `00-13` | 18 | bank 1 bit 6 clear |
| `00-13` | 30 | bank 1 bit 1 set and bit 6 clear |
| `04-08` | 64 | required |
| `04-08` | 65 | bank 1 bit 1 clear |
| `05-09` | 6 | required |
| `05-0e` | 0, 1 | required |

ID 30's predicate is incompatible with the enforced Leon A scenario. It cannot bypass WrongGame or publish visits. The other six get synthetic departed-state/acquisition-reversal regressions with the generated rules. All room fields besides these presence lists and the new claim ID remain identical to baseline, including sound hashes, exclusions, scenario variant selection, quantities and event awards. Generated floor plans remain byte-identical. The shared proof adds no per-room or named-item exception.

`replay.py` compares 169 untouched prior native captures with baseline metadata through the unchanged reader. Both sides explicitly seed the target visit as a test history; this is not a claim that earlier captures originally visited that room. Statuses, item identities/quantities/counts, doors, pose, heading, floor and visits agree. Only unknown item states covered by the seven rules can become Uncollected, with corresponding Incomplete/reason changes. There are 147 such seeded-history differences and 22 identical results. Non-Display observations retain identical unpublished trackers. Complete adjusted trackers and Display publications compare equal.

Every restoration begins without item verdicts or door knowledge. The pre-collection departed capture and its two earlier departed restores now retain ID 65 Uncollected and the room Incomplete. The six original native F1 restore pairs still have identical full 2 MiB RAM and now produce identical revised publications. These are previous actual restores replayed here, not six new F1 loads. `capture-replay.json` and `fresh-publications.json` preserve the comparisons.

## RE2-COLLECT-016: bounded script writes preserve scenario proofs

Status: Confirmed for the listed source `00-13` selected-item collection, repeat collection, ordinary departure/reentry and exact earlier/later savestate observations. The shared command admission has copied-native effect checks and synthetic guards. Arbitrary actor pointers, untested script contexts and campaign readiness are not confirmed.

Follow-up, 2026-10-24: [RE2-COLLECT-017](#re2-collect-017-fixed-context-gates-fresh-pickup-presence) resolves the remaining departed-presence gap in the same tested source; the earlier Unknown observations below remain the baseline evidence.

The human explicitly approved auditing the blockers beginning at opcode `0x60`, implementing only safe shared rules, regenerating metadata, validating current/departed and rewind behavior and committing verified changes. The baseline is `177c573`. Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, full build commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Owned `ROOM1130.RDT`, source `00-13`, is 188928 bytes, SHA256 `862fef343291ae422cf75358a40f63f5d002ba518427ff51938f209157a76e0d`.

Private reproducibility files are under `/tmp/re-map-re2-scenario-source0013-20261024/`. Raw RAM, game sources, native checkpoints and black game-display captures remain outside Git. `native_effects.py`, `native-effects.json`, `metadata-changes.json`, `replay.py`, `capture-replay.json`, `native_audit.py`, `native-audit.json`, `fresh-publications.json` and the `native/` logs distinguish copied execution, source analysis, replay and new gameplay.

### Failure and bounded command admission

Before the fix, source `00-13` retains incompatible ID 30 and cannot resolve ID 145's conflicting types after departure. With ID 18 already collected, collecting ID 145 still leaves the source Unknown. The whole-source scenario proof rejects opcode `0x60` at offset 4264, then independently encounters `0x1D`, `0x37`, `0x0D` and `0x0E`. A process-local diagnostic forecast is retained in `hypothesis-blockers.json`. That forecast was a hypothesis, not production proof.

The audit compares each native instruction span with the pinned owned executable and runs the original instructions in copied RAM. Dispatch entries are unsigned 32-bit little-endian PS1 virtual pointers at `0x8009F94C + 4 * opcode`, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. The additional admitted effects are:

| Opcode and native handler | Observed effect and admission bound |
| --- | --- |
| `0x60`, `0x8005487C` through return at `0x800549B0` | Selects an actor's linked record through its pointer at actor `+0x16C`. Category 1/2 use pointer words at `0x800C7F40`/`0x800C7F44`; category 3 uses `0x800C7F48 + 4 * signed_index`. Reject other categories and enemy indices outside 0 through 31. The native callee `0x800169E8` preserves the high byte of two record words and changes their low RGB bytes, mask `0x00FFFFFF`. Four packet halfwords update the linked record, then the task PC advances 14 bytes. |
| `0x1D`, `0x80050B50` through return at `0x80050BA4` | Reads a 16-bit little-endian counter at `0x800CC918 + 2 * unsigned_index`, mask `0xFFFF`. Writes its low byte, or its halfword when packet byte 3 is nonzero, to `packet + 4 + unsigned_byte_2`. This modifies later script bytes. Permit only a complete write inside the same procedure's `0x32` numeric operands at bytes 2 through 7, or `0x40` numeric operands at bytes 4 through 7. Halfword targets must be aligned. Opcode, identity, selector, predicate, branch, pointer and loop-control operands remain forbidden. |
| `0x37`, `0x80052BDC` through return at `0x80052C1C` | Reads the loaded RDT pointer word at `0x800C6494`, then the relocated SCA pointer at RDT `+0x20`. It updates the 16-bit little-endian flags of SCA record `unsigned_index + 1`, at record `+8`, with `(old & 0x007F) \| (operand & 0xFF80)`. Admission checks an aligned declared collision section, its header-inclusive record count, the index, the full extent before the next section and absence of script overlap. |
| `0x0D`, `0x800503B8` through return at `0x80050468`; `0x0E`, `0x80050558` through return at `0x800505D0` | FOR and ENDFor update task-local loop counters, bounds and PC. A zero FOR count skips the body. Admission requires paired forward local bounds, at most four nested loops, no mixed WHILE/DO loops, bounded paired IF/Endif frames with supported tests, no Else, no early end/return within a body and no IF/jump edge crossing a body boundary. Every decoded creator in the whole FOR procedure stays possible, including creators behind a zero count or fixed-false guard. |

All addresses in this entry are PS1 virtual addresses, not host-process addresses. Pointer/task words use width 4 and mask `0xFFFFFFFF`; the named record fields and counters use width 2 and mask `0xFFFF`. Byte selectors and indices use width 1 and mask `0xFF`. Source offsets are byte offsets into the owned RDT. The existing decoder requires the complete 100-byte RDT header and in-file sections before scenario interpretation.

Numeric destinations of the self-modifying command were separately audited. Native `0x32` at `0x80052220` expands three signed packet halfwords into the selected actor's position fields. Native `0x40` at `0x80053258` updates that actor's motion/destination fields. Neither numeric operand range introduces a creator, changes a fixed context bit or modifies bytecode. In `ROOM1130.RDT`, counter copies at offsets 4414/4418 target the last two halfwords of `0x40` at 4422. The other five copies target a later `0x32` coordinate. No blanket self-modifying opcode whitelist was added.

The copied audit runs 18592 cases: 240 for `0x60`, 13824 for `0x1D`, 4096 for `0x37`, 256 for FOR, 48 for ENDFor and 64 each for the numeric destination handlers. Every store stays in the declared task, script operand, actor/linked-record, collision or native stack range. The complete player and save blocks remain unchanged after each explicit setup. Result digest: `bdd08bc4d9d14859435b181e27119149b029fd5654bafef96fd7170c6d678c7d`. The copied source capture is SHA256 `37fb193ffc3bf3141f94348c415aec2b1198eaaf20e728d19ec385c205c6cb35`.

An earlier top-level-return probe for halfword `0x1D` raised a CPU exception at PC zero after the expected stores. `native-probe-rejections.json` preserves that rejected probe. This is not a native game failure or a confirmed emulator-library defect. The accepted audit stops before each top-level JR; the SCA handler's original delay-slot PC store is executed separately. Explicit copied actor/linked-record and RDT/SCA pointers do not establish safety for arbitrary malformed pointers.

The final generated delta excludes ID 30 in `00-13` and selects ID 145's creator at offset 3822, type 20, quantity 15. ## RE2-COLLECT-017: fixed context gates fresh pickup presence

Status: Confirmed for all four compatible new presence rules in the listed pickup lifecycles: source `00-13`, the [follow-up in source `04-02`](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-04-02-2026-10-24), the [follow-up in source `01-10`](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-01-10-2026-10-24) and the [follow-up in source `01-08`](#native-follow-up-for-generated-source-01-08-2026-10-24). Each covers remaining-item departure, native collection, repeated collection, reentry and exact earlier/later restores. The first two reach clearance; the third retains an unrelated Unknown check and the fourth retains a required remaining item. Confirmation is limited to these named conditions. The general stable-flag whitelist is unchanged.

Follow-up, 2026-10-24: [RE2-COLLECT-018](#re2-collect-018-scripted-interactions-preserve-declared-item-identity) resolves the remaining ID 87 presence gap in source `01-10`. The earlier Unknown observations below remain historical baseline evidence.

The human approved the named shared fixed-context presence fix, including audit, regeneration, hidden native validation and verified commits. Baseline: `5a6b04b`. Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Owned `ROOM1130.RDT` remains 188928 bytes, SHA256 `862fef343291ae422cf75358a40f63f5d002ba518427ff51938f209157a76e0d`. Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-fixed-context-presence-20261024/`; no raw RAM, owned source, RE2 save or game display enters Git.

### Failure and shared proof boundary

RE2-COLLECT-016 resolves ID 145's identity but its departed state remains Unknown before acquisition. The selected creator at RDT offset 3822 lies behind the existing mode predicate, bank 0 bit 25, which the general stable-flag contract deliberately omits. A read-only diagnostic admits that predicate and forecasts sufficient presence under bank `0/25 = 1`, `1/1 = 0` and `1/6 = 0`. `forecast.json` labels this as a hypothesis, not an implemented proof.

The fix makes the existing whole-source scenario analysis report whether it preserved the fixed context, separately from its absent IDs and selected creator variants. A source can prove preservation even when neither item-result list contains anything. Every unsupported effect, conflicting direct/aliased/raw writer, incomplete source, invalid control path or exhausted analysis bound rejects the context proof. Existing native-effect admission and reachability rules are unchanged. Construction asserts that rejected proofs contain no item results.

Generation records a required Boolean `fixed_context_preserved` per source. Only a successful whole-source result admits the profile's already enforced fixed predicates to ordinary pickup presence analysis. The same shared flag-set implementation validates the exported clauses when loading. Missing/non-Boolean claims, fixed-context clauses without a successful claim, incompatible item-proof claims and incomplete certified sources fail with explicit diagnostics. Existing independent stable-flag and event-award proofs retain their contracts; event-award flag admission is unchanged. No general mode-bit stability whitelist, item-name exception or cached presence is introduced.

The canonical fixed masks remain system word PS1 virtual `0x800C7CE4`, unsigned 32-bit little endian, mask/value `0x00000040`, and scenario word `0x800C7D04`, same width/order, mask `0xC2000000`, value zero. The former is bank 0 bit 25. RE2-COLLECT-011's two independent native initializations establish the observed pre-gameplay initializer for this pinned context; its opposite-mode and indirect-writer limits remain. The runtime gate and coherence check already enforce those values before publishing. Earlier savestates and supported captures reread them.

The owned native flag-table base is PS1 virtual `0x8009FD38`; entries are unsigned 32-bit little-endian pointers, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Native check opcode `0x21` at `0x80050BF0` through JR at `0x80050C48` reads the bank/bit and computes its Boolean comparison, writing only task PC at context `+0x1C` to packet `+4`. Native writer `0x22`, `0x80050C50` through JR `0x80050D08`, performs clear/set/toggle for operations 0/1/7. These instructions are matched against the pinned owned executable. `native_effects.py` runs 80 copied checks and 48 copied writes, including mode/scenario bits, full-word seeds, both predicate results and a constructed alias. All stores stay in the expected task/flag words; every other byte in the full player/save blocks stays unchanged. Result digest: `299b7e0183c816922ab454cfb76cb7ee1a03471d4ef2fe70c99fa3e1803a1cb9`. The copied input RAM SHA256 is `1e4bf9595bf8af5e97db6840f81aa52cec29465360e24f69c53e25f4b6820ff2`.

Bank 99 is an explicitly constructed copied alias to the system word, not an owned native bank declaration. An initial writer probe raised a CPU exception at PC zero after its expected stores; it is rejected in `native-probe-rejection.json`, without a game-failure or library-defect claim. Accepted writer probes stop before the top-level jump/return and execute the original final store separately. A toggle replay initially retained a stop hook at that same store, so it executed nothing; that setup error is retained in `native-probe-corrections.json`. The corrected complete audit passes. No copied probe changes the running emulator or establishes arbitrary malformed-pointer safety.

Twenty-four of 125 sources pass the whole-source context proof. The new field records rejection for the other 101; it does not remove their existing independent item proofs. Every prior item declaration, source hash, exclusion, selected identity, unconditional item list and floor-plan byte remains unchanged. Five new conditional records are generated: `00-13` ID 145, `01-02` ID 116, `01-08` ID 216, `01-10` ID 219 and `04-02` ID 21. ID 116's mode-clear clause contradicts the enforced context and it remains excluded. ID 21's complementary sufficient clauses reduce to Required. The other new clauses require the existing bit-set context. These are generated proof results, not five new native pickup validations.

## RE2-COLLECT-018: scripted interactions preserve declared item identity

Status: Confirmed for ID 87 in source `01-10` on the named revision during pre-activation current/departed observations, native callback restoration, two collections, departure/reentry and exact earlier/later restores. The shared rule remains Candidate beyond these listed observations. The other new generated proof concerns a file and adds no collectible or clearance claim.

The human approved this named shared fix, conservative native audit, regeneration, hidden validation and verified commits. Baseline: `c86fc32`. Game: Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Owned `ROOM2100.RDT` is 84128 bytes, SHA256 `ba14d87ec3a680baf2fad6f1503242006e91637e553943112e1f642dd46cc121`. Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-scripted-presence-20261024/`; owned sources, raw RAM, checkpoints and suppressed display captures remain outside Git.

### Failure and shared lifetime rule

The source unconditionally declares ID 87, type 21, amount seven, at RDT byte offset 4912 in slot three. While its acquisition flag is clear, resets at 4942 and 5026 replace that interaction with kind five and payload `FF 00 18 02 00 00`. The live descriptor therefore contains a script binding instead of the original item operands. Both the old guard and the baseline native observations leave the item Unknown, including after departure.

Main procedure `17:2` starts at 4992 with `Aot_reset` packet `46 03 02 31 15 00 07 00 57 00`. Before any other command, it restores slot three to the same type 21/acquisition ID 87. It then performs its native actor operation and `Aot_on`. The reset is activation of the declared item, not a replacement collection identity. This extends [RE2-COLLECT-003](#re2-collect-003-scripted-activation-preserves-collection-identity), whose historical rule rejects every same-slot script reset.

The shared lifetime guard admits only canonical kind-five automatic main-procedure bindings, with selector `FF 00`, marker `18` and zero reserved tail. Their named procedure must exist and its entry command must reset that exact slot to ordinary item kind two with the same type/acquisition identity. Quantity may change under the existing explicit amount-alternative contract. Padding, calls or predicates before the restoring command, missing procedures, noncanonical bindings, conflicting creators, other identity resets, self-modifying `Copy` commands anywhere in the source and incomplete catalogs withhold the proof. Matching callbacks do not establish creator reachability: startup and sufficient-condition analysis still have to prove the original declaration. No item name, source ID exception, new flag stability rule or cached identity is introduced.

The owned kind-five handler uses the task selector and procedure byte. Restricting the other payload bytes to the known binding format is a conservative source-proof boundary, not a claim that every ignored native byte is validated by the engine. Existing zeroed inactive resets, matching item resets, generic-slot exclusions and later mutation checks keep their contracts. Metadata schema, profile identity, history format and runtime acquisition interpretation are unchanged.

The initial implementation missed a later bytecode mutation. A synthetic room schedules a `Copy` that changes the callback entry's kind byte from two to five after startup. Before that write, the initial rule incorrectly exported the item as required; applying the audited native copy effect removes the proof. `self-modification-reproduction.json` retains this reproduction. The final rule withholds only the new script-restoration proof in sources containing self-modifying opcode `0x1D`; ordinary unrelated item proofs keep their existing contracts. Two regressions target the callback kind and amount while retaining an unrelated required item. This is a conservative immutability boundary, without adding another native write-target interpreter. The guard leaves every owned generated byte and all native/replay outcomes unchanged.

### Native contracts and copied execution

Instruction words and pointer entries below are unsigned 32-bit little endian, mask `0xFFFFFFFF`; byte operands use `0xFF`, and item payload halfwords use `0xFFFF`. Addresses are PS1 virtual addresses. Copied execution maps those addresses to explicit RAM offsets with mask `0x1FFFFF`; no host-process address is used as a game address.

Native `Aot_reset`, `0x800517D4..0x80051844`, selects its registered descriptor through `0x800C66C8`, an array of 32 four-byte pointers. It writes kind, low interaction flags and six payload bytes. Original flag `0x80` preserves the geometry form, selecting payload offset twelve or twenty. Native `Aot_on`, `0x80051848..0x800518DC`, dispatches through the descriptor's current kind using the pointer table at `0x8009F87C`. Kind five enters `0x8004E42C..0x8004E468`; unless inhibited by PS1 virtual word `0x800C7D08`, unsigned 32-bit little endian, mask `0x02000000`, it passes the payload's selector and main-procedure byte to the already audited scheduler at `0x8004FAA0`. RE2-COLLECT-003 records that scheduler contract. Source instruction bytes match the pinned owned executable.

`native_effects.py` checks 72 copied reset cases over slots 0/3/31, both geometry forms, kinds 0/2/5, flags 0/49 and two distinct payloads. Only expected descriptor fields and task PC change; player/save blocks remain identical. Two copied kind-five checks verify the inhibit predicate and native argument reads for selector 255/procedure two, with the expected return-address stack store. The scheduler itself is not executed by these probes.

The first whole-handler probe is rejected. Its recorded trace executes taken target `0x8005181C` twice in that copied setup and writes the payload at an unexpected offset. `native-probe-rejection.json` preserves it without a game or library defect claim. Accepted probes execute the original straight instruction segments, compute guards natively and select the source-inspected continuation explicitly. Branch, return and scheduler instructions are not executed. `native-probe-correction.json` records this limit; these copied cases are not gameplay confirmation or malformed-pointer safety evidence.

### Generated changes and regressions

Regeneration changes only the research reference and two required-ID lists: source `01-10` adds ID 87, and `01-08` adds ID 25, type 113. The latter is above the profile's file threshold and remains excluded from collectible completion. Every creator, quantity, source hash, fixed-context claim, exclusion, selected identity, conditional presence rule, event award, door and floor-plan byte stays unchanged. `metadata-changes.json` retains the comparison.

Twenty-two metadata cases cover immediate identity restoration, changed quantity, malformed/nonautomatic bindings, reserved bytes, missing or delayed callbacks, foreign slot/type/acquisition identity, another interaction kind, later mutation, conflicting creators, incomplete catalogs, conditional creators and uncalled creators. Two generated-profile regressions cover overwritten current descriptors and departed rooms through acquired/uncollected reversals, fresh trackers and synthetic clearance when all three relevant items are acquired. These regressions establish interpretation contracts, not native full-clearance evidence. Scoped Ruff checks pass; the focused suite passes 779 tests in 7.20 seconds and the final full suite passes 2420 in 104.58 seconds.

Untouched RAM from 410 prior captures is interpreted through baseline/current metadata. Full Tracker comparison permits only ID 87's Unknown-to-Uncollected state and derived completion/reason changes. Exactly 28 comparisons change, all in source `01-10` for that identity. Other quantities, item labels, source records, doors, positions and history agree. Replay histories start empty or accumulate only chronological accepted captures in each private case. This adds no gameplay or source coverage.

### Hidden native validation and restore evidence

Both achievement sections are disabled before boot and every slot preparation; overrides are absent, cards unmounted and audio muted. Startup loads the second selected-identity case's earlier current checkpoint, SHA256 `4cb1dfea8db69b42d1645810ffc0a6c710c0cb3b0b2f08e4e87484858fd298e9`, reproducing its full RAM SHA256 `05c7eb89c7f8151c994dd6030172ee25cf51ee7d08f3b62b1fe754e4fd49d6d4`. Every complete RAM image and full map publication equals its corresponding original. ## RE2-LIVE-004: source 01-0c leaves pickup 207 unresolved

Status: Confirmed for source acceptance, absent slot ten and unchanged inventory/acquisition in the listed native Leon A observations. Scenario-wide absence is Candidate. Native pickup identity, quantity and collection remain unvalidated.

This evidence-only pass uses `91eb721` without changing interpretation, metadata, profiles, tests or UI. Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM20C0.RDT` is 122004 bytes, SHA256 `80f0e7f5b2024e31c7f0b27cfb052ea581899bb2e987f788c9b5eb439ede9c44`.

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-source010c-id207-native-20261024/`. `source-identity.json`, `source-route.json`, `native-audit.json`, `fresh-publications.json`, `native/core-checkpoints.json`, `native/core-restore-pairs.json` and `native/native-f1-actions.json` retain source provenance, observations, reconstructed publications and actual native actions. Owned sources, raw RAM, checkpoints and suppressed display captures remain outside Git.

### Creation conditions and observed blocker

The catalog declares ID 207 in slot ten with conflicting types 21 and 29, quantities seven and thirty-six. Declarations at RDT offsets 7414/7440 use X/Z rectangle `-900/-900`, size `1800/1800`, with activation-floor byte `0xC2`; declarations at 7534/7598 use X/Z rectangle `-11900/-25100`, size `1800/1800`. These are source operands, not an observed inventory award. The generated source has a complete candidate list but no fixed-context proof, selected identity or presence proof for ID 207.

In startup procedure `17:0`, the 7414/7440 declarations sit inside the branch requiring bank 1 bit 1 set, bank 4 bit 128 clear and bank 6 bit 200 clear. The 7534/7598 declarations require bank 6 bit 200 set. Bank 1 bit 0 chooses the type within each branch. The observed Leon A state has bank 1 bits 0/1 clear and bank 6 bit 200 clear, so neither creation branch runs. Procedure `17:1` can set bank 6 bit 200 only inside a branch requiring bank 1 bit 1 set. Procedure `17:2` contains type-selecting slot-ten resets, but no creator. This local source inspection explains these observations; it does not prove a scenario-wide exclusion across every writer and game state.

All addresses here are PS1 virtual, not host-process addresses. Status bank 1 is `0x800C7D04`, unsigned 32-bit little endian, masks `0x80000000` and `0x40000000` for bits 0/1. ### Failure and shared identity rule

[RE2-COLLECT-019](#re2-collect-019-preserved-context-prunes-inactive-startup-branches) resolves an independent known item's departed presence but leaves pickup 207 generic. The preserved Leon A context reaches creator 7534, slot ten, type 21 and amount seven. In procedure `17:2`, reset 7870 writes the same identity and quantity. Reset 7884, type 29 and amount thirty-six, is unreachable under the fixed character predicate. The old variant proof nevertheless rejected every acquisition ID appearing in any ordinary reset.

The new variant proof checks all reachable resets related to a candidate's possible slots or acquisition ID. Matching ordinary resets must preserve a selected slot, type, acquisition identity and that slot's published quantity set. Canonical all-zero inactive resets preserve identity without proving availability. Script, foreign-ID, foreign-slot and other conflicting resets withhold selection. Uncalled procedures remain independent roots, and opaque loops contribute every command even for a zero count. A reachable ordinary creator is still required; reset-only definitions cannot manufacture a selected catalog offset.

The decoder already unions matching reset quantities into each creator's `amounts`. This implementation reuses that set. A different matching reset amount can retain the identity while leaving quantity unreconstructed; it does not collapse the set to the original amount. The blanket all-reset acquisition-ID guard remains unchanged for absence proofs. Raw declarations and lifetime/presence analysis keep their existing contracts. In particular, no presence rule or exclusion is added for 207.

### Native reset bounds and copied evidence

All addresses here are PS1 virtual. Instructions, pointers and task PC use unsigned 32-bit little-endian words, mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Reset slot/kind/flags are bytes, mask `0xFF`; payload type/amount/acquisition are little-endian halfwords, mask `0xFFFF`. Copied execution maps addresses with mask `0x1FFFFF`, never as host-process addresses.

Pinned handler `0x800517D4..0x80051844` reads its slot with signed `LB`, indexes the 32-entry pointer registry at `0x800C66C8`, writes the interaction kind, and ORs packet flags with the old geometry bit `0x80`. It chooses payload offset twelve or twenty using the old bit, then stores three halfwords and advances task PC by ten. There is no native index bound or null-pointer check. Whole-source context proof now rejects reset slots outside `0..31` and packets independently setting geometry bit `0x80`. These are conservative proof bounds, not new runtime error handling or a claim that every rejected form fails in gameplay.

`native_effects.py` runs 504 copied straight-segment cases across raw slot bytes 0/10/31/32/127/128/255, both original geometry forms, kinds 0/2/5, flags 0/49/128/177 and three relevant payloads. The signed pointer lookup and six expected stores are checked. All player/save bytes remain identical after explicit copied setup. Exactly 126 constructed cases show an interpreted-payload mismatch when flags change geometry; 42 use ordinary-item kind two. `geometry-rejection.json` retains one such case. The interpreted-payload comparison is a probe check, not an executed item consumer.

Original branch guards run natively, but straight continuations are selected explicitly under the previously recorded copied-runner limitation. Branches, top-level returns and the item-award handler are not executed. These checks do not establish gameplay in another scenario, arbitrary malformed-pointer safety or a native seven-unit award for this pickup.
