## RE2-FLAG-001: source 01-0c presence flag provenance

Status: Candidate for initialization and a whole-playthrough flag invariant. One boot supplies a directly observed initialization store, without repeated independent boot validation. Confirmed only for the listed exact native restores and unchanged Unknown publications across distinct earlier/later states. Pickup 207 remains Unknown. This audit establishes no scenario-wide absence, new item presence, award or clearance rule.

The human approved an evidence-only audit of bank six bit 200, its initialization, script/native/aliased writers and hidden savestate validation. Production changes require a separate increment. Baseline: `54fafc8`. Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Owned `PL0/RDT/ROOM20C0.RDT` is 122004 bytes, SHA256 `80f0e7f5b2024e31c7f0b27cfb052ea581899bb2e987f788c9b5eb439ede9c44`.

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-presence-flag207-audit-20261024/`. `source_audit.py`, `source-audit.json`, `native-contracts.txt`, `native-source.json`, `native-call-candidates.json`, `copied-flags.json`, `native/boot-flag-watch.json`, `native/core-checkpoints.json`, `native/native-f1-actions.json`, `native-audit.json`, `audit-corrections.json` and `focused-tests.log` retain source, copied execution, boot, restore and verification provenance. Owned files, disassembly, raw RAM, states and suppressed captures remain outside Git.

### Storage and initialization

Addresses here are PS1 virtual, never host-process addresses. Instruction words and pointers are four-byte little-endian values with full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Bank six starts at `0x800CC9A8`, width 32 bytes. Bit 200 occupies unsigned little-endian word `0x800CC9C0`, width four, mask `0x00800000`, equivalently byte `0x800CC9C2`, width one, mask `0x80`. Counter index 85 aliases the latter address as a little-endian signed halfword, with target-bit mask `0x0080`. Index 84 overlaps the lower half of the same word but not the target bit. Native counters start at `0x800CC918` and have no bounds check in the audited assignment handler.

The executable's initial target word is zero. Its header BSS address and size are both zero, which alone says nothing about initialization. Entry `0x80073BD8` runs an explicit word-clear loop over `0x800BBE58..0x800E0AB0`, end exclusive. A fresh hidden boot without a savestate starts at PC `0xBFC00000`. A write watch first records a BIOS-segment store through alias `0xA00CC9C0`, then the game's store at `0x80073BE8`, stopped at PC `0x80073BEC`. Register V0 is the target address, V1 is the loop end and the stored word is zero. Captured startup instructions equal the owned executable. Alias comparison uses the PS1 RAM mask `0x1FFFFF`; the BIOS store is retained separately from the game initialization observation. This single boot does not prove all later initialization or restore paths.

### Bounded writer inventory

The source audit rereads and hash-checks all 125 catalog RDTs. The native flag-pointer table at `0x8009FD38` supplies all 36 bank entries and agrees with every configured bank. The only decoded direct mutation of the target is packet `22 06 C8 01`, source `01-0c`, procedure `17:1`, RDT offset 7828. Its enclosing first check requires bank one bit one set, outside the required Leon A context. The three direct target checks occur at offsets 7402, 7484 and 7820. This does not establish that native code elsewhere cannot set the bit.

Native command pointers at `0x8009F94C` pin the destination operands used for the alias scan. Assignment/copy opcodes `0x24/0x25` use byte one; immediate calculation `0x26` uses byte three; variable calculation `0x27` uses byte two; member-to-counter `0x3D` uses signed byte one. Among 3924 such decoded commands, none writes either overlapping halfword. Opcode `0x35` writes an actor member, not a counter. Actor-member aliases through a different selected pointer are not covered by this counter scan.

Native actor construction copies declaration byte nine to actor offset `0x1CE`, a one-byte field with mask `0xFF`. Helper `0x80018910` passes that field to shared flag setter `0x80072ADC`. It selects bank six when saved stage `0x800CC948`, signed little-endian width two, mask `0xFFFF`, is below three and status word `0x800C7D04`, unsigned little-endian width four, mask `0x02000000`, is clear; otherwise it selects bank seven. None of 755 decoded actor declarations carries value 200. Native actor copying and later field mutation still need a complete lifetime proof before this supports a global invariant.

The shared set, clear and check helpers start at `0x80072ADC`, `0x80072B04` and `0x80072B30`. The audit retains 26 direct set/clear call contexts as leads, not an exhaustive argument or indirect-call proof. Six input words through each copied set/clear segment preserve unrelated bits and change only the target mask. Four synthetic counter-85 assignments demonstrate the halfword alias, including value `0x0080` setting the target and `0x8000` leaving it clear. These sixteen cases execute original straight instruction segments without branches or returns; they never write the emulator and are not gameplay. A suspected bulk-copy lead at `0x800561AC` is rejected because its destination ends before `0x800CC918`, excluding the target.

The initial audit helper incorrectly rejected read-only bank-eleven checks because the profile omits that native bank. The corrected scan uses the pinned native table, including bank eleven at `0x800DE5FC`, and finds no unknown bank packets. The opcode-to-counter correction and rejected copy lead are retained. Neither is a production defect.

### Native restores and limits

Achievements are disabled in both sections before boot and every state load. Game overrides are absent, cards unmounted, sound muted and the black shader enabled. Suppressed captures before boot execution, after boot and after restores are checked without showing game content. Boot initialization RAM never enters gameplay exploration history.

Seven actual F1 actions reuse four native F2 checkpoints from [RE2-COLLECT-019](#re2-collect-019-preserved-context-prunes-inactive-startup-branches). Every full 2 MiB RAM image equals its original. After the initial current-source observation and an actual departed-source restore establish both private visits, six complete map publications equal the originals recorded in [RE2-COLLECT-020](#re2-collect-020-reachable-resets-preserve-selected-pickup-identity). Current and baseline publications are identical throughout. Fresh production history-file save/close/reopen reproduces each map from visits alone, with exactly two sources and two regions and no cached item, completion or door verdict.

Bit 200 and pickup 207's acquisition remain clear, and its slot-ten pointer remains null while current. Pickup 207 still publishes its selected identity and source quantity as Unknown. Its distinct acquisition word is `0x800CCA00`, unsigned little-endian width four, mask `0x00010000`; its registry pointer is `0x800C66F0`, width four, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Independent pickup 170 rewinds correctly across earlier/later checkpoints, using `0x800CC9FC`, unsigned little-endian width four, mask `0x00200000`. This reverses a neighboring acquisition, not the target presence flag. No target set-to-clear restore, availability, native award or collection is observed. The room never becomes Cleared.

No guest memory writes, position preparation, new collection or F2 occurs. The gameplay fixtures retain health 28576 at the initial earlier checkpoint and handgun quantity 255, the inherited infinite-magazine sentinel; later health remains checkpoint-specific. Cleanup stops only the agent emulator and closes port 2346. Settings and selected slot restore byte for byte, SHA256 `39d5fd0e6b687c65d33de20066c14288f3e4d05ba253fd0dab024e2875646829` and `45e26a2484778ddeb86fb6b0d3aed4640be3f1a9c913167a1da5b06307b2483d`. User emulator, saves and histories are untouched.

All 168 tracked production, data, profile, test and tool hashes remain unchanged. The focused RE2/history/logging suite passes 829 tests in 7.16 seconds. Local documentation links and whitespace checks pass. No new full-suite run or external model review is claimed for this evidence-only pass. Runtime source coverage stays 68 of 125, 54.4%; these sources were already accepted. Offline writer counts do not measure readiness or overall RE1 parity. Renderer and controls are unchanged, so the README screenshot is retained.

The remaining gap is a whole-playthrough write invariant covering indirect native calls, actor lifetimes/member aliases, dynamic overlays, auxiliary scripts, bulk/whole-word save writes and initialization after entry. The current source inventory and clear-state restores cannot substitute for that proof. Production presence and absence metadata remain unchanged; normal memory-card persistence and other scenarios remain untested.

## RE2-FLAG-002: native actor flag initialization and shared setter provenance

Status: Confirmed for the two listed native constructor stores and six exact restore pairs. Candidate for cloning, member stores, loaded-code dispatch and any whole-playthrough invariant. No native actor-to-bank flag store is observed. Pickup 207 remains Unknown; no production presence, absence, collection or clearance rule changes.

The human approved an evidence-only native actor-flag audit for pickup 207, initialization/copying/indirect writes, hidden validation, isolated history and a verified evidence commit. Baseline: `3723f98`. Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. DuckStation is `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. Work starts on 2026-10-24 and finishes on 2026-10-25.

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-actor-flag207-audit-20261024/`. `source-contracts.json`, `member-store-table.json`, `copied-actor.json`, `overlay-call-leads.json`, `overlay-ram-bindings.json`, `loaded-store-leads.json`, `native/actor-constructor-observations.json`, `native/actor-flag-observations.json`, `native-audit.json` and the correction records retain source, copied execution, native observations and limits. Owned assets, disassembly, RAM, savestates and suppressed images stay outside Git.

### Constructor, copy and member contracts

Code/data addresses here are PS1 virtual, never host-process addresses. Instructions and pointers are unsigned four-byte little-endian values, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Actor field `+0x1CE` is one byte, mask `0xFF`. Pinned constructor segment `0x800539D8..0x800539E4`, end exclusive, copies packet byte nine into this field. Cloning function `0x8001A914` contains the byte-preserving copy at `0x8001AC98..0x8001ACA4`. This segment alone does not prove the complete clone lifecycle or later callbacks.

The 44-entry member-store table at `0x80010F00` dispatches through `0x80052330`. None of its selected stores overlaps `+0x1CE` when the supplied base is a genuine actor. This includes the halfword at `+0x1CC`, which ends before the flag byte. It does not establish pointer identity or exclude indirect aliases through another base.

Shared helper `0x80018910` passes the actor's byte to flag setter `0x80072ADC`. Its bank-six/bank-seven selection remains the source contract in [RE2-FLAG-001](#re2-flag-001-source-01-0c-presence-flag-provenance). The copied checks cover all 256 constructor byte inputs, all 256 clone byte inputs, four values for each member store and 24 explicitly selected dispatch cases. All 712 cases execute original straight instruction segments; native guard results select continuations explicitly. Branches and returns are not executed. Synthetic flag values 200 and 255 are test inputs, not observed source actors. These cases use Unicorn `2.1.4` and Capstone `5.0.7` and never write the emulator.

A halfword-store lead at `0x8007C45C` also uses displacement `0x1CE`. Its base comes through `0x800A35D8`, whose owned-executable value and selected native observations are `0x1F801C00`. The resulting destination is `0x1F801DCE`, outside actor RAM. This rejects an actor-field interpretation in those observations, without claiming a complete lifetime proof for that base pointer.

### Loaded-code leads

The aligned raw scan covers 213 owned executable, BIN, DIE, DO2, EMS, PLD and RDT files. Its 64 calls to `0x80018910` and sixteen calls to `0x8001A914` occur in `PL0/PLD/CDEMD0.EMS`, 11104256 bytes, SHA256 `58fc57262e4c6b06caaf853d56f6a457c043f0daa122344e4fb599ba8178a743`. These are candidate instruction matches, not a decoded control-flow graph or exhaustive disc-code inventory.

Eleven aligned calls to the flag helper appear in the initial captured RAM. Each 44-byte context matches owned EMS bytes. Only one has a unique source match; repeated identical source contexts preserve ambiguity. The raw store-displacement scan over `0x80100000..0x8011A480`, end exclusive, finds adjacent-field stores but no direct-displacement flag-byte store. It includes possible data and does not cover computed bases, relocated code elsewhere, bulk copies or indirect dispatch. No native actor-to-bank write is confirmed from these leads.

### Native construction in two sources

Two native passages, `01-0c` to `01-0a` and back, produce constructor write-watch stops at PC `0x800539E4`, immediately after the flag store. Source `01-0a` writes value 32 to `0x8011E926`, actor `0x8011E758` plus `0x1CE`. Its owned declaration is at RDT offset 2622 in `PL0/RDT/ROOM20A0.RDT`, SHA256 `409068af4d7aeaaec6ce4450d50894cdbdeec492388e5e3431ad0ab5092521fe`. Source `01-0c` writes value 91 to `0x8011E782`, actor `0x8011E5B4` plus `0x1CE`, from declaration 7232 in `PL0/RDT/ROOM20C0.RDT`, SHA256 `80f0e7f5b2024e31c7f0b27cfb052ea581899bb2e987f788c9b5eb439ede9c44`. Both observed packets match the owned bytes and native register values. Constructor/copy/member/helper code windows match the pinned executable in the selected captures.

The initial binding helper incorrectly reused loaded base `0x8011A480` from another capture. The corrected check reads the mandatory profile pointer at `0x800C6494` independently from each constructor-stop RAM image. Both stops use `0x8011A65C`; observed packet pointers equal this captured base plus the owned offsets. `source-binding-correction.json` retains the rejected assumption. No production source-binding change is made.

Current-source checkpoint restores have actor flag bytes 91, 92, 93 and 148. Departed-source restores have 32, 33, 34, 124, 205 and 206. The 32-entry pointer table starts at `0x800C7F48`; active records require the unsigned little-endian four-byte actor word's mask `0x00000001`. Unused entries share an inactive record and are not additional actors. No active actor in the accepted captures carries flag 200. These observations do not establish a whole-playthrough actor lifetime invariant.

### Negative attempts, restore validation and cleanup

Three bounded bank-six watches observe no change. The first input chord is rejected before application because Computer Use permits only one non-modifier key. The owned test instance is stopped, its native aim binding changes from `Keyboard/P` to `Keyboard/Shift`, and a second isolated boot uses the same checkpoint. A native input probe records `0x48` under the supported modifier chord, but neither that attempt nor one position-prepared attempt produces the sought flag write. Firing, a kill and shared-setter execution are not established. These negative attempts are retained and do not support absence. The audit then switches to the constructor observations above.

Three position/facing preparations use only the established nine fields after observed native free control `0x00010001`; no control word is patched. Two preparations support the passages. No actor, flag, inventory, script, story, key, mode, Y or floor edit occurs. No new health/ammunition aid, native collection or F2 occurs. The initial fixture inherits health 28576 and handgun quantity 255, the native infinite-magazine sentinel. Native contact changes health; later restores retain each checkpoint's own value.

Twelve accepted captures reconstruct complete maps after production visits-only history save/close/reopen, with exactly two actual sources and two regions. Current and baseline maps agree throughout. Six actual F1 actions reuse four native F2 checkpoints from [RE2-COLLECT-019](#re2-collect-019-preserved-context-prunes-inactive-startup-branches). Every restored full 2 MiB RAM image and complete map equals its original. Actor fields and a neighboring acquisition rewind correctly. Pickup 207's presence bit remains clear, its acquisition remains clear and its slot-ten pointer remains null while current. It retains its selected identity and source quantity with state Unknown, and the room never reports Cleared. Target addresses, widths and masks remain those in RE2-FLAG-001.

One second-constructor read is Retry and preserves the preceding complete publication. The first constructor-stop RAM has no publication because the private helper incorrectly restored a current room from visit history without a heading. The corrected helper retains visits and leaves current unset, matching production history restoration, until an accepted read supplies live state. `capture-helper-correction.json` preserves this harness failure; production is unchanged.

The capture named `reentered-settled` is an accepted early-startup frame, not proof of completed startup or native free control. Its item-170 slot-five pointer at `0x800C66DC`, unsigned little-endian width four, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`, is null. The shared source proof still reports that item Uncollected and keeps the room Incomplete. The initial oracle wrongly demanded Available in every current frame. Only this named expectation is corrected; all six original RAM/map comparisons stay exact. `availability-oracle-correction.json` retains the distinction. A post-F1 ScreenCaptureKit observation error is recovered through full intended-checkpoint RAM equality and fresh accessibility state, without repeating F1 blindly. Its fifth load is journaled against its actual fifth slot preparation after preparation of the next slot; provenance retains that ordering explicitly.

Both achievement sections are disabled before each boot and load. Overrides are absent, cards unmounted, audio muted and the black shader verified through suppressed before/restart/after captures. Cleanup stops both owned processes and closes port 2346. Original settings and selected slot restore byte for byte, SHA256 `39d5fd0e6b687c65d33de20066c14288f3e4d05ba253fd0dab024e2875646829` and `45e26a2484778ddeb86fb6b0d3aed4640be3f1a9c913167a1da5b06307b2483d`. User emulator, saves and histories are untouched.

All 168 tracked production/data/profile/test/tool hashes remain unchanged. The focused RE2/history/logging suite passes 829 tests in 7.02 seconds. Local documentation links and whitespace checks pass. No new full-suite run or external model review is claimed for this evidence-only increment. Runtime room-source coverage remains 68 of 125, 54.4%, separately from item outcomes and overall RE1 parity. Renderer and controls are unchanged, so the README screenshot is retained.

The audit confirms the selected constructor path and narrows the clone/member/loaded-code leads. It does not close all actor lifetimes, computed-pointer aliases, auxiliary creation, other loaded code or native/bulk writers of the presence flag. Production presence and absence rules remain unchanged; other scenarios and normal memory-card persistence remain untested.

## RE2-FLAG-003: remaining native flag-call argument bounds

Status: Candidate source/copy contracts. Twenty-four of the 25 selected calls exclude the target under their audited function-entry and argument contracts. One signed-room call still needs a native lifetime invariant. This supplies no new presence, absence, collected-item or clearance result. Pickup 207 remains Unknown.

The human approved a finite evidence-only audit of the remaining 25 native set/clear calls, their target reachability and index bounds, validation and an evidence commit. Baseline: `f93046d`. Resident Evil 2 USA Disc 1, Leon A, SLUS-00421, executable `SLUS_004.21`, SHA256 `1cd618bc4390db4bd60735b0db1e91534f6134ede56fae454eb580be2aa686ef`. Existing native evidence uses DuckStation `0.1-12083-g00e299795`, commit `00e2997958d62de79d352cd680ec0c1ad95d491c`. No emulator is launched in this pass.

Private evidence is under `/tmp/re-map-re2-nonactor-flag207-audit-20261025/`. `call-contexts.txt`, `call_audit.py`, `call-audit.json`, `copied-calls.json`, `replay.json` and `focused-tests.log` retain source locations, bounds, copied execution, reused native provenance and checks. Owned code, raw RAM and states remain outside Git. The human questioned the practical value of the preceding audits; this pass uses existing restore captures and makes no new playability or coverage claim.

### Finite call inventory

Addresses here are PS1 virtual unless identified as RAM offsets. Instructions, pointers, native helper indices and words are unsigned four-byte little-endian values, full mask `0xFFFFFFFF`. Byte loads have mask `0xFF`; signed/unsigned halfwords have raw mask `0xFFFF`. The target remains word `0x800CC9C0`, width four, mask `0x00800000`. [RE2-FLAG-001](#re2-flag-001-source-01-0c-presence-flag-provenance) records its bank-six bit-200 identity and the shared set/clear helpers `0x80072ADC` and `0x80072B04`.

The owned executable contains 26 aligned direct JAL encodings to those helpers. This audit covers all 25 except actor helper call `0x8001896C`, already considered in [RE2-FLAG-002](#re2-flag-002-native-actor-flag-initialization-and-shared-setter-provenance). Several remaining callers also handle actor state; the exclusion is of that one helper, not all actor-related code. The inventory does not close indirect/tail calls, other caller stores, overlays, bulk writes or arbitrary pointer lifetimes.

| Argument family | Calls | Source contract |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Constant base/index | 10 | `0x8003831C`, `0x80038B7C`, `0x80063C70`, `0x80063C7C`, `0x800640B0`, `0x80064128`, `0x800647F4`, `0x800649DC`, `0x800666AC`, `0x800666BC`; none selects the target word/mask |
| Masked byte | 1 | `0x8004E13C`: base `0x800CCB60`, index `0..63` |
| Unsigned halfword | 3 | `0x8004EE2C`: base `0x800CCB60`; `0x8006DB2C` and `0x8006E35C`: bases `0x800CC9E8` or `0x800CCA08`; all indices `0..65535` remain beyond the target without wrapping |
| Unsigned byte | 9 | Eight calls from `0x8005EBE0` through `0x80062220` use base `0x800CC9A0` and actor byte `+0x1CB`; `0x80067D58` uses base `0x800CCA18` and a table byte; indices `0..255` exclude the target |
| Bounded selector result | 1 | `0x80064BA8`: base `0x800CCA34`, selector result `0..19` |
| Signed room plus prefix | 1 | `0x80064B88`: base `0x800CCA38`; synthetic negative room values can alias the target |

The base formations, load widths and executed delay slots are pinned in the copied checks. The `0x800666BC` set path retains base `0x800CC980`; the zero assignment after the alternate clear path's jump does not execute on that set path. A linear scan across both branches would misidentify the argument.

The eight byte-indexed calls at base `0x800CC9A0` are not bounded by the logical eight-byte bank width. Larger byte indices can address the first 192 bits of adjacent bank six. Target bit 200 would require index 264 relative to this base, beyond LBU's maximum 255. This excludes this target without asserting that logical banks are isolated.

The [native area selector](#re2-map-001-native-leon-floor-selectors-and-numeric-contours), `0x80069FC0..0x8006A2B4`, returns `0..19` under its source contract. Its guarded seven-entry jump table is pinned at `0x80011928`. The source check covers all 31 direct transfers into the common epilogue, including the two NOP-delay cases whose preceding executed instructions define the result, plus the zero-result fallthrough. The body has only its guarded table jump and final return as JR instructions, and its external calls are the read-only flag predicate. Normal function entry, intact pinned code/tables and callee-saved register preservation remain explicit assumptions.

### Signed-index alias and bounded validation

Call `0x80064B88` reads saved room `0x800CC94A` as a signed little-endian halfword and adds a stage prefix. The guarded table at `0x800117A4` selects prefixes `0,30,58,72,89,99,123`. All 125 catalog source IDs exclude the target. That catalog check is not a proof of every native value stored in the room halfword.

With prefix zero and synthetic room `-952`, the unsigned helper index is `0xFFFFFC48`. Logical shift/index arithmetic computes address `0xA00CC9C0` with mask `0x00800000`, aliasing the target's RAM offset `0xCC9C0`. The other six prefixes have corresponding negative-room witnesses. RAM normalization is applied only after recognizing a RAM address range, not to arbitrary device addresses. This shows why a bank-base comparison alone is insufficient. No native invalid-room occurrence or gameplay defect is observed, and no tracker exception or game patch follows.

There are 2391 copied original argument/helper cases, including every byte value at each of the nine unmasked-byte call sites, halfword boundary values on both stage arms, selector results and seven negative-room alias witnesses. Selected straight instruction fragments include relevant call delay slots. Calls, branches and returns are not executed in this set; native guard results select explicit source-inspected continuations. Caller side effects outside those fragments are not audited as part of the helper argument result. The initial handwritten selector-exit count of 29 is rejected; enumeration and pinned delay-slot checks establish 31. No production code changes.

Six existing native restore pairs from RE2-FLAG-002 are rechecked for complete original RAM/map equality and checkpoint hashes. Current production interpretation replays each through the copied visits-only history, without adding visits or changing history bytes. All 25 call windows, selector code and both jump tables match the owned executable in these captures. Six whole-selector copied runs reach the explicit return boundary, return within `0..19` and write only their copied stack. Saved rooms are positive and the target remains Unknown. These are reuse/replay and copied-execution checks, not six new F1 actions or gameplay observations.

All 168 tracked production/data/profile/test/tool hashes remain unchanged. The focused RE2/history/logging suite passes 829 tests in 7.07 seconds. Local documentation links and whitespace checks pass. No new full-suite run or external model review is claimed. No emulator settings, user saves/history or game memory are changed. Runtime room-source coverage remains 68 of 125, 54.4%; no new room, item or door outcome is resolved. Renderer and controls are unchanged, so the README screenshot is retained.

The finite call inventory is now classified, but the signed-room lifetime and other native/indirect writers still prevent a global absence proof. This audit ends without a production rule or playability gain; continued whole-game tracing is not implied by these results.
